true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md\", \"content\": \"# ROADMAP\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:53 UTC | 更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\\n\\n## 项目目标\\n\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\n\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\n\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\n\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\n\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\n\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\n\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\n\\n## Operational 判据\\n\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\n\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\n\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\n\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` 与 `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse`；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。\\n\\nH1 success for exp_002：positive-correlation conditions 的 oracle-global-worse rate 至少比 negative-correlation controls 高 20 percentage points，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0；bias-bound diagnostic 对 oracle-global-worse conditions 的 Spearman rho >= 0.50，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。estimated-global 结果必须与 oracle-global 分开报告。\\n\\nH2 success for Stage 1：low expert-label budget 下，partial pooling 在 GATE RMSE 上优于 global correction，`partial_pooling_minus_global` bootstrap 95% CI upper bound < 0；相对 per-subgroup independent correction 满足 5% relative GATE RMSE non-inferiority。PEHE 和 mean subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics。\\n\\n## 短期计划\\n\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\n4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup variables、可用 split 与复现实验权限；未确认前不把 real-data 写成已完成结果。\\n\\n## Experiment Plan / Matrix 映射\\n\\n当前最低覆盖目标：\\n- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\\n- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\\n- `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation` → `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`, `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`。\\n- `plan_004_realdata_feasibility` → `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY`。\\n\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\n\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\n- oracle global correction vs estimated global correction vs uncorrected\\n\\nStage 1 correction：\\n- uncorrected\\n- global confusion correction\\n- arm-specific correction\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\n\\nStage 2 inference：\\n- naive CI\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\n- bootstrap CI\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\n\\nStage 3 allocation：\\n- uniform allocation\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\n- joint criterion\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\n\\nReal-data validation：\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\n\\n## 工程产出\\n\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\n\\n## 决策规则\\n\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Memory\\n\\n## 项目定位\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\n\\n## 执行约束\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\n\\n## 领域参考\\n\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\n\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\n\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\n\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\n\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\n\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\n\\n## 首批实验协议\\n\\n### 统一评测输出\\n\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\n\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\n\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\n\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\n\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\"。\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\n\\n## Real-data 可行性\\n\\n- Rohingya corpus 判定：**部分可支撑**。Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao 2025 的 Rohingya/Bengali host interview corpus 足以支撑 real-data validation 设计与 https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；arXiv DOI https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2309.17147；代码库 https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\n- 数据可得性与文本：论文报告 2,407 个 open-ended interviews、32,463 个 question-answer pairs；访谈在 Bengali/Rohingya dialect 中进行，转写为 Bengali，再经 Google Translate 翻译成 English 并人工校正后编码。Data Availability Statement 明确 code public，transcripts 因 privacy 不能公开下载，anonymised version will be available upon request。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246。\\n- Schema 与 labels：coding tree 包括 aspiration、ambition、navigational capacity 等层级，论文主分析聚焦 19 个 binary codes；789 transcripts 由两名 annotators 在 Atlas.ti 中 human-coded，并经 reviewer/weekly resolution process 处理分歧。LLM annotation 覆盖 GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4o mini、Llama-2 13B、Llama-3 8B，使用 code-specific instructions、few-shot prompting 和 chain-of-thought prompting；public repo 未发现 record-level Rohingya labels/LLM outputs。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；repo tree https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\n- Subgroup religious education 等变量。World Bank summary 也明确 LLM errors are not random with respect to refugee status, gender, education。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；World Bank newsletter 项目定位\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\n\\n## 执行约束\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\n\\n## 领域参考\\n\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\n\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\n\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\n\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\n\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\n\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\n\\n## 首批实验协议\\n\\n### 统一评测输出\\n\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\n\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\n\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\n\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\n\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\"。\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\n\\n## Real-data 可行性\\n\\n- Rohingya corpus 判定：**部分可支撑**。Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao 2025 的 Rohingya/Bengali host interview corpus 足以支撑 real-data validation 设计与 differential LLM misclassification 论证，但公开材料不足以直接复现实验；需要向作者/World Bank 申请 anonymized transcripts、record-level expert labels、record-level LLM labels 与 split IDs。来源：SAGE DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；arXiv DOI https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2309.17147；代码库 https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\n- 数据可得性与文本：论文报告 2,407 个 open-ended interviews、32,463 个 question-answer pairs；访谈在 Bengali/Rohingya dialect 中进行，转写为 Bengali，再经 Google Translate 翻译成 English 并人工校正后编码。Data Availability Statement 明确 code public，transcripts 因 privacy 不能公开下载，anonymised version will be available upon request。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246。\\n- Schema 与 labels：coding tree 包括 aspiration、ambition、navigational capacity 等层级，论文主分析聚焦 19 个 binary codes；789 transcripts 由两名 annotators 在 Atlas.ti 中 human-coded，并经 reviewer/weekly resolution process 处理分歧。LLM annotation 覆盖 GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4o mini、Llama-2 13B、Llama-3 8B，使用 code-specific instructions、few-shot prompting 和 chain-of-thought prompting；public repo 未发现 record-level Rohingya labels/LLM outputs。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；repo tree https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\n- Subgroup variables：refugee status、eldest child's gender、parent's years of education 被论文显式用于 Figure 8/9 regressions；quantitative table 还列出 female household head、household head age、number of children、parent religious education 等变量。World Bank summary 也明确 LLM errors are not random with respect to refugee status, gender, education。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；World Bank newsletter https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf。\\n- Split 与 license/IRB：paper 描述 R2/R3 sampling、expert-coded sample、iQual 80/20 test split 与 3-fold CV，但公开 split IDs 待核验；iQual code 是 MIT license，CBPS baseline Dataverse DOI https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB metadata reports CC0 1.0，World Bank HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33 是 public-use files。Transcript license、data-use agreement、IRB/consent constraints 待核验。来源：https://github.com/worldbank/iQual/blob/main/LICENSE.md；https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB；https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33。\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/fields_and_label_sources/data_schema_summary/exp_id=null/source=partial_support: public paper documents 2,407 interviews, 32,463 QA pairs, binary QA/interview code labels, 789 expert-coded transcripts, and LLM annotation methods; raw transcripts and record-level labels require request. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; repo https://github.com/worldbank/iQual`\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/expert_split_available/expert_split_status/exp_id=null/source=partial_support_pending_access: expert-coded sample and train/test/CV design are described, but public split IDs and record-level labels were not found. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246`\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/subgroup_variables_available/subgroup_variable_coverage/exp_id=null/source=support_for_variables_pending_linkage: refugee status, eldest-child gender, and parent education are explicitly used in the paper; public CBPS survey metadata supports host/camp and household/individual variables, but transcript-label linkage requires restricted/anonymized data access. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; CBPS https://macmillan.yale.edu/refugee/coxs-bazar-panel-survey; HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33`\\n\\n## 执行事件记录\\n\\n- [2026-05-31] `art_20260531_baseline_protocol`。失败原因是非关键收尾命令在非 git repo 目录执行 `git status` / `git diff` 报错，不影响 baseline/protocol 内容。默认不重跑同一调研 Worker；除非 Director 要求更严格 source audit。\\n- [2026-05-31] {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_23","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"nl -ba repro/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/run_gpu_dgp_grid.py | sed -n '1,460p'\"","aggregated_output":"     1\t#!/usr/bin/env python3\n     2\t\"\"\"GPU-vectorized DGP grid for EC-HTE H1.\n     3\t\n     4\tThis script is designed to support a pending-only proposal and a short dry run.\n     5\tThe formal run must wait for Director approval and GPU allocation.\n     6\t\"\"\"\n     7\t\n     8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     9\t\n    10\timport argparse\n    11\timport itertools\n    12\timport json\n    13\timport math\n    14\timport time\n    15\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n    16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n    17\tfrom typing import Any\n    18\t\n    19\timport torch\n    20\t\n    21\t\n    22\tEXP_ID = \"exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\"\n    23\t\n    24\t\n    25\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n    26\tclass Condition:\n    27\t    condition_id: str\n    28\t    correlation_direction: str\n    29\t    misclassification_heterogeneity: str\n    30\t    cate_heterogeneity: str\n    31\t    k: int\n    32\t    n: int\n    33\t    expert_budget: int\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tdef parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:\n    37\t    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    38\t    parser.add_argument(\"--config\", required=True)\n    39\t    parser.add_argument(\"--output-dir\", required=True)\n    40\t    parser.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cuda\", choices=[\"cuda\", \"cpu\"])\n    41\t    parser.add_argument(\"--max-replications\", type=int, default=None)\n    42\t    parser.add_argument(\"--limit-conditions\", type=int, default=None)\n    43\t    parser.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\")\n    44\t    return parser.parse_args()\n    45\t\n    46\t\n    47\tdef load_config(path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    48\t    with open(path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as handle:\n    49\t        return json.load(handle)\n    50\t\n    51\t\n    52\tdef condition_grid(config: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Condition]:\n    53\t    conditions: list[Condition] = []\n    54\t    keys = [\n    55\t        config[\"misclassification_heterogeneity\"],\n    56\t        config[\"cate_heterogeneity\"],\n    57\t        config[\"subgroup_counts\"],\n    58\t        config[\"n_values\"],\n    59\t        config[\"expert_budgets\"],\n    60\t        config[\"correlation_directions\"],\n    61\t    ]\n    62\t    for mis_h, cate_h, k, n, budget, direction in itertools.product(*keys):\n    63\t        condition_id = (\n    64\t            f\"corr={direction}|mis={mis_h}|cate={cate_h}|k={k}|\"\n    65\t            f\"N={n}|B={budget}\"\n    66\t        )\n    67\t        conditions.append(\n    68\t            Condition(\n    69\t                condition_id=condition_id,\n    70\t                correlation_direction=direction,\n    71\t                misclassification_heterogeneity=mis_h,\n    72\t                cate_heterogeneity=cate_h,\n    73\t                k=int(k),\n    74\t                n=int(n),\n    75\t                expert_budget=int(budget),\n    76\t            )\n    77\t        )\n    78\t    return conditions\n    79\t\n    80\t\n    81\tdef evenly_spaced(items: list[Condition], count: int) -> list[Condition]:\n    82\t    if count <= 0:\n    83\t        return []\n    84\t    if count >= len(items):\n    85\t        return items\n    86\t    if count == 1:\n    87\t        return [items[0]]\n    88\t    step = (len(items) - 1) / (count - 1)\n    89\t    return [items[int(round(i * step))] for i in range(count)]\n    90\t\n    91\t\n    92\tdef balanced_limit_conditions(conditions: list[Condition], limit: int) -> list[Condition]:\n    93\t    positives = [c for c in conditions if c.correlation_direction == \"positive\"]\n    94\t    negatives = [c for c in conditions if c.correlation_direction == \"negative\"]\n    95\t    pos_count = (limit + 1) // 2\n    96\t    neg_count = limit // 2\n    97\t    selected = evenly_spaced(positives, pos_count) + evenly_spaced(negatives, neg_count)\n    98\t    return selected[:limit]\n    99\t\n   100\t\n   101\tdef cate_values(k: int, heterogeneity: str, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:\n   102\t    if heterogeneity == \"high\":\n   103\t        vals = torch.linspace(0.15, 1.15, k, device=device)\n   104\t    else:\n   105\t        vals = torch.linspace(0.35, 0.65, k, device=device)\n   106\t    return vals - vals.mean() + 0.5\n   107\t\n   108\t\n   109\tdef error_rates(\n   110\t    k: int,\n   111\t    mis_heterogeneity: str,\n   112\t    cate: torch.Tensor,\n   113\t    direction: str,\n   114\t    device: torch.device,\n   115\t) -> torch.Tensor:\n   116\t    if mis_heterogeneity == \"high\":\n   117\t        low, high = 0.04, 0.36\n   118\t    else:\n   119\t        low, high = 0.10, 0.18\n   120\t    ordered = torch.linspace(low, high, k, device=device)\n   121\t    order = torch.argsort(torch.abs(cate), descending=False)\n   122\t    rates = torch.empty(k, device=device)\n   123\t    if direction == \"positive\":\n   124\t        rates[order] = ordered\n   125\t    else:\n   126\t        rates[order] = torch.flip(ordered, dims=[0])\n   127\t    return rates\n   128\t\n   129\t\n   130\tdef transition_matrix_from_rates(rates: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n   131\t    k = int(rates.numel())\n   132\t    matrix = torch.zeros((k, k), device=rates.device)\n   133\t    off_diag = rates / max(k - 1, 1)\n   134\t    matrix[:] = off_diag[:, None]\n   135\t    diag_idx = torch.arange(k, device=rates.device)\n   136\t    matrix[diag_idx, diag_idx] = 1.0 - rates\n   137\t    return matrix\n   138\t\n   139\t\n   140\tdef global_transition_matrix(p_error: torch.Tensor, k: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:\n   141\t    rates = torch.full((k,), p_error.clamp(0.0, 0.95), device=device)\n   142\t    return transition_matrix_from_rates(rates)\n   143\t\n   144\t\n   145\tdef sample_noisy_labels(z_true: torch.Tensor, rates: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n   146\t    k = int(rates.numel())\n   147\t    device = z_true.device\n   148\t    error = torch.rand_like(z_true.float()) < rates[z_true]\n   149\t    shift = torch.randint(1, k, z_true.shape, device=device)\n   150\t    z_alt = (z_true + shift) % k\n   151\t    return torch.where(error, z_alt, z_true)\n   152\t\n   153\t\n   154\tdef observed_gate(y: torch.Tensor, treatment: torch.Tensor, labels: torch.Tensor, k: int) -> torch.Tensor:\n   155\t    estimates = []\n   156\t    for group in range(k):\n   157\t        mask = labels == group\n   158\t        treated = mask & (treatment == 1)\n   159\t        control = mask & (treatment == 0)\n   160\t        if treated.any() and control.any():\n   161\t            estimates.append(y[treated].mean() - y[control].mean())\n   162\t        else:\n   163\t            estimates.append(torch.tensor(float(\"nan\"), device=y.device))\n   164\t    out = torch.stack(estimates)\n   165\t    if torch.isnan(out).any():\n   166\t        global_ate = y[treatment == 1].mean() - y[treatment == 0].mean()\n   167\t        out = torch.where(torch.isnan(out), global_ate, out)\n   168\t    return out\n   169\t\n   170\t\n   171\tdef correct_gate(observed: torch.Tensor, transition: torch.Tensor, ridge: float) -> torch.Tensor:\n   172\t    k = transition.shape[0]\n   173\t    lhs = transition.T + ridge * torch.eye(k, device=transition.device)\n   174\t    return torch.linalg.solve(lhs, observed)\n   175\t\n   176\t\n   177\tdef gate_rmse(estimate: torch.Tensor, truth: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n   178\t    return torch.sqrt(torch.mean((estimate - truth) ** 2))\n   179\t\n   180\t\n   181\tdef bias_bound_score(rates: torch.Tensor, cate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n   182\t    centered_rate = rates - rates.mean()\n   183\t    centered_cate = torch.abs(cate) - torch.abs(cate).mean()\n   184\t    return torch.mean(torch.abs(centered_rate * centered_cate))\n   185\t\n   186\t\n   187\tdef rankdata(values: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n   188\t    order = torch.argsort(values)\n   189\t    ranks = torch.empty_like(values, dtype=torch.float32)\n   190\t    ranks[order] = torch.arange(values.numel(), device=values.device, dtype=torch.float32)\n   191\t    return ranks\n   192\t\n   193\t\n   194\tdef spearman(x: torch.Tensor, y: torch.Tensor) -> float:\n   195\t    if x.numel() < 2 or torch.std(x) == 0 or torch.std(y) == 0:\n   196\t        return float(\"nan\")\n   197\t    rx = rankdata(x.float())\n   198\t    ry = rankdata(y.float())\n   199\t    rx = rx - rx.mean()\n   200\t    ry = ry - ry.mean()\n   201\t    denom = torch.sqrt(torch.sum(rx**2) * torch.sum(ry**2))\n   202\t    if denom == 0:\n   203\t        return float(\"nan\")\n   204\t    return float(torch.sum(rx * ry) / denom)\n   205\t\n   206\t\n   207\tdef quantile(values: list[float], q: float) -> float:\n   208\t    if not values:\n   209\t        return float(\"nan\")\n   210\t    sorted_values = sorted(values)\n   211\t    idx = min(max(int(math.floor(q * (len(sorted_values) - 1))), 0), len(sorted_values) - 1)\n   212\t    return float(sorted_values[idx])\n   213\t\n   214\t\n   215\tdef bootstrap_metrics(\n   216\t    condition_rows: list[dict[str, Any]],\n   217\t    iterations: int,\n   218\t    seed: int,\n   219\t    device: torch.device,\n   220\t) -> dict[str, float]:\n   221\t    pos = [r for r in condition_rows if r[\"correlation_direction\"] == \"positive\"]\n   222\t    neg = [r for r in condition_rows if r[\"correlation_direction\"] == \"negative\"]\n   223\t    if not pos or not neg:\n   224\t        return {\n   225\t            \"primary_ci_lower\": float(\"nan\"),\n   226\t            \"primary_ci_upper\": float(\"nan\"),\n   227\t            \"spearman_ci_lower\": float(\"nan\"),\n   228\t            \"spearman_ci_upper\": float(\"nan\"),\n   229\t        }\n   230\t\n   231\t    generator = torch.Generator(device=device)\n   232\t    generator.manual_seed(seed)\n   233\t    primary_samples: list[float] = []\n   234\t    spearman_samples: list[float] = []\n   235\t    rows = condition_rows\n   236\t    for _ in range(iterations):\n   237\t        pos_idx = torch.randint(0, len(pos), (len(pos),), generator=generator, device=device)\n   238\t        neg_idx = torch.randint(0, len(neg), (len(neg),), generator=generator, device=device)\n   239\t        row_idx = torch.randint(0, len(rows), (len(rows),), generator=generator, device=device)\n   240\t        pos_rate = torch.tensor(\n   241\t            [pos[int(i)][\"oracle_global_worse_rate\"] for i in pos_idx],\n   242\t            device=device,\n   243\t        ).mean()\n   244\t        neg_rate = torch.tensor(\n   245\t            [neg[int(i)][\"oracle_global_worse_rate\"] for i in neg_idx],\n   246\t            device=device,\n   247\t        ).mean()\n   248\t        primary_samples.append(float(pos_rate - neg_rate))\n   249\t        x = torch.tensor([rows[int(i)][\"bias_bound_score\"] for i in row_idx], device=device)\n   250\t        y = 0.14, 0.18),\n    63\t        outcome_sd=1.0,\n    64\t    )\n    65\t\n    66\t\n    67\tdef make_confusion_matrix(error_rates: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n    68\t    \"\"\"Return C[z, g] = P(Z=z | G=g) with uniform off-diagonal errors.\"\"\"\n    69\t\n    70\t    k = len(error_rates)\n    71\t    if np.any(error_rates < 0) or np.any(error_rates >= (k - 1) / k):\n    72\t        raise ValueError(\"error rates must be in [0, (k - 1) / k)\")\n    73\t    confusion = np.zeros((k, k), dtype=float)\n    74\t    for g, error in enumerate(error_rates):\n    75\t        confusion[:, g] = group_probs: np.ndarray\n    82\t) -> np.ndarray:\n    83\t    \"\"\"Return the oracle pooled/global confusion model used for global correction.\"\"\"\n    84\t\n    85\t    pooled_error = float(np.dot(error_rates, group_probs))\n    86\t    k = len(error_rates)\n    87\t    pooled_error = float(np.clip(pooled_error, 0.0, 0.98 * (k - 1) / k))\n    88\t    confusion = np.full((k, k), 103\t\n   104\tdef corrected_gate(observed_gate_z: np.ndarray, posterior_w: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n   105\t    \"\"\"Invert tau_Z = W tau_G via a stable pseudo-inverse.\"\"\"\n   106\t\n   107\t    return np.linalg.pinv(posterior_w, rcond=1e-10) @ observed_gate_z\n   108\t\n   109\t\n   110\tdef simulate_dataset(config: DGPConfig, n: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:\n   111\t    tau = np.asarray(config.tau_values, dtype=float)\n   112\t    probs = np.asarray(config.group_probs, dtype=float)\n   113\t    errors = np.asarray(config.error_rates, dtype=float)\n   114\t    k = len(tau)\n   115\t    true_g = rng.choice(k, size=n, p=probs)\n   116\t    treatment = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, size=n)\n   117\t    baseline = 0.35 * true_g + 0.25 * np.sin(true_g)\n   118\t    outcome = baseline + tau[true_g] * treatment + rng.normal(0.0, config.outcome_sd, n)\n   119\t\n   120\t    confusion = make_confusion_matrix(errors)\n   121\t    noisy_z = np.empty(n, dtype=int)\n label) & (treatment == 0)\n   136\t        if treated.sum() == 0 or control.sum() == 0:\n   137\t            estimates[label] = np.nan\n   138\t        else:\n   139\t            estimates[label] = outcome[treated].mean() - outcome[control].mean()\n   140\t    if np.any(np.isnan(estimates)):\n   141\t        fill = float(np.nanmean(estimates))\n   142\t        estimates = np.nan_to_num(estimates, nan=fill)\n   143\t    return estimates\n   144\t\n   145\t\n   146\tdef estimate_global_confusion(\n   147\t    true_g: np.ndarray,\n   148\t    noisy_z: np.ndarray,\n true_g[idx]\n   159\t    sampled_z = noisy_z[idx]\n   160\t    group_counts = np.bincount(sampled_g, minlength=k).astype(float)\n   161\t    mismatches = np.bincount(sampled_g[sampled_g != sampled_z], alpha))\n   164\t    pooled_error = float(np.clip(pooled_error, 0.0, 0.98 * (k - 1) / k))\n   165\t    confusion = np.full((k, k), pooled_error / (k - 1), dtype=float)\n   166\t    np.fill_diagonal(confusion, 1.0 - pooled_error)\n   167\t    return confusion\n   168\t\n   169\t\n   170\tdef metric_record(estimate: np.ndarray, truth: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, float]:\n   171\t oracle_global_confusion = global_confusion_from_pooled_error(errors, probs)\n   198\t    oracle_global_posterior = posterior_true_given_noisy(oracle_global_confusion, probs)\n   199\t \"estimate\": float(estimate_g),\n   231\t                    \"true_tau\": float(truth_g),\n   232\t                    \"bias\": float(estimate_g - truth_g),\n   233\t                    \"abs_bias\": float(abs(estimate_g - truth_g)),\n   234\t                    \"squared_error\": float((estimate_g - truth_g) ** 2),\n   235\t                }\n   236\t            )\n   237\t    return metric_rows, group_rows\n   238\t\n   239\t\n   240\tdef bootstrap_ci(\n   241\t    values: Iterable[float], rng: np.random.Generator, n_bootstrap: int\n   242\t) -> tuple[float, float]:\n   243\t    arr = np.asarray(list(values), dtype=float)\n   244\t    if arr.size == 0:\n   245\t        return float(\"nan\"), float(\"nan\")\n   246\t    if arr.size == 1 or n_bootstrap <= 0:\n   247\t        value = float(arr.mean())\n   248\t        return value, value\n   249\t    idx = rng.integers(0, arr.size, size=(n_bootstrap, arr.size))\n   250\t    boot = arr[idx].mean(axis=1)\n   251\t    return float(np.quantile(boot, 0.025)), float(np.quantile(boot, 0.975))\n   252\t\n   253\t\n   254\tdef summarize(metrics_rows: list[dict[str, object]], args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, object]:\n   255\t    rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed + 999_999)\n   256\t    method_summaries: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, float]]] = {}\n   257\t    rows_by_method = {\n   258\t        method: [row for row in metrics_rows if row[\"method\"] == method] for method in METHODS\n   259\t    }\n   260\t    for method in METHODS:\n   261\t        frame = rows_by_method[method]\n   262\t        method_summaries[method] = {}\n   263\t        for metric in [\n   264\t            \"gate_rmse\",\n   265\t            \"mean_subgroup_cate_bias\",\n   266\t            \"max_subgroup_cate_bias\",\n   267\t        ]:\n   268\t            values = np.asarray([float(row[metric]) for row in frame], dtype=float)\n   269\t            ci_low, ci_high = bootstrap_ci(values, rng, args.bootstrap)\n   270\t            method_summaries[method][metric] = {\n   271\t                \"value\": float(values.mean()),\n   272\t                \"ci_low\": ci_low,\n   273\t                \"ci_high\": ci_high,\n   274\t            }\n   275\t\n   276\t    def paired_diff(left: str, right: str, metric: str) -> dict[str, float]:\n   277\t        by_rep: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\n   278\t        for row in metrics_rows:\n   279\t            rep = int(row[\"replication\"])\n   280\t            by_rep.setdefault(rep, {})[str(row[\"method\"])] = float(row[metric])\n   281\t        values = np.asarray(\n   282\t            [method_values[left] - method_values[right] for method_values in by_rep.values()],\n   283\t            dtype=float,\n   284\t        )\n   285\t        ci_low, ci_high = bootstrap_ci(values, rng, args.bootstrap)\n   286\t        return {\"value\": float(values.mean()), \"ci_low\": ci_low, \"ci_high\": ci_high}\n   287\t\n   288\t    comparisons = {\n   289\t        \"oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse\": paired_diff(\n   290\t            METHOD_ORACLE_GLOBAL, METHOD_UNCORRECTED, \"gate_rmse\"\n   291\t        ),\n   292\t        \"estimated_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse\": paired_diff(\n   293\t            METHOD_ESTIMATED_GLOBAL, METHOD_UNCORRECTED, \"gate_rmse\"\n   294\t        ),\n   295\t        \"estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse\": paired_diff(\n   296\t            METHOD_ESTIMATED_GLOBAL, METHOD_ORACLE_GLOBAL, \"gate_rmse\"\n   297\t        ),\n   298\t        \"oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_mean_subgroup_cate_bias\": paired_diff(\n   299\t            METHOD_ORACLE_GLOBAL, METHOD_UNCORRECTED, \"mean_subgroup_cate_bias\"\n   300\t        ),\n   301\t        \"oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_max_subgroup_cate_bias\": paired_diff(\n   302\t            METHOD_ORACLE_GLOBAL, METHOD_UNCORRECTED, \"max_subgroup_cate_bias\"\n   303\t        ),\n   304\t    }\n   305\t\n   306\t    primary = comparisons[\"oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse\"]\n   307\t    gate_supports_h1 = primary[\"value\"] > 0.0 and primary[\"ci_low\"] > 0.0\n   308\t    opposite_secondary = [\n   309\t        name\n   310\t        for name in [\n   311\t            \"oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_mean_subgroup_cate_bias\",\n   312\t            \"oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_max_subgroup_cate_bias\",\n   313\t        ]\n   314\t        if comparisons[name][\"value\"] < 0.0\n   315\t    ]\n   316\t    mixed = bool(gate_supports_h1 and opposite_secondary)\n   317\t    if mixed:\n   318\t        conclusion = \"mixed\"\n   319\t    elif gate_supports_h1:\n   320\t        conclusion = \"success\"\n   321\t    else:\n   322\t        conclusion = \"negative\"\n   323\t\n   324\t    return {\n   325\t        \"exp_id\": EXP_ID,\n   326\t        \"config\": SCENARIO_KEY,\n   327\t        \"primary_metric\": \"gate_rmse\",\n   328\t        \"secondary_diagnostics\": [\n   329\t            \"mean_subgroup_cate_bias\",\n   330\t            \"max_subgroup_cate_bias\",\n   331\t        ],\n   332\t        \"n_replications\": len({int(row[\"replication\"]) for row in metrics_rows}),\n   333\t        \"n_per_replication\": int(args.n),\n   334\t        \"expert_budget\": int(args.expert_budget),\n   335\t        \"bootstrap_replications\": int(args.bootstrap),\n   336\t        \"method_metrics\": method_summaries,\n   337\t        \"comparisons\": comparisons,\n   338\t        \"success_rule\": {\n   339\t            \"oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse_gt_0\": bool(primary[\"value\"] > 0.0),\n   340\t            \"bootstrap_95_ci_lower_gt_0\": bool(primary[\"ci_low\"] > 0.0),\n   341\t            \"gate_rmse_supports_h1\": bool(gate_supports_h1),\n   342\t            \"opposite_secondary_diagnostics\": opposite_secondary,\n   343\t            \"mixed\": mixed,\n   344\t            \"conclusion\": conclusion,\n   345\t        },\n   346\t    }\n   347\t\n   348\t\n   349\tdef matrix_line(\n   350\t    target_id: str,\n   351\t    row: str,\n   352\t    col: str,\n   353\t    metric_key: str,\n   354\t    source: str,\n   355\t    metric: dict[str, float],\n   356\t    n_replications: int,\n   357\t) -> dict[str, object]:\n   358\t    return {\n   359\t        \"target_id\": target_id,\n   360\t        \"row\": row,\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_26","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"python3 - <<'PY'\nimport json, statistics, itertools\nfrom pathlib import Path\np=Path('artifacts/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/formal/condition_metrics.jsonl')\nrows=[json.loads(line) for line in p.read_text().splitlines()]\nprint('rows',len(rows))\nprint('fields', sorted(rows[0]))\n# global means by direction\nfor d in ['positive','negative']:\n    rs=[r for r in rows if r['correlation_direction']==d]\n    print('dir',d,'n',len(rs),'worse_mean',sum(r['oracle_global_worse_rate'] for r in rs)/len(rs),'oracle_minus_unc_mean',sum(r['oracle_global_gate_rmse']-r['uncorrected_gate_rmse'] for r in rs)/len(rs),'est_minus_oracle_mean',sum(r['estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse'] for r in rs)/len(rs))\n# group by dims\ndef mean(xs): return sum(xs)/len(xs)\nfor dims in [('misclassification_heterogeneity',),('cate_heterogeneity',),('k',),('n',),('expert_budget',),('misclassification_heterogeneity','cate_heterogeneity'),('misclassification_heterogeneity','cate_heterogeneity','k')]:\n    print('\\\\nGROUP',dims)\n    groups={}\n    for r in rows:\n        key=tuple(r[d] for d in dims)\n        groups.setdefault(key,[]).append(r)\n    for key,rs in sorted(groups.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0])):\n        pos=[r for r in rs if r['correlation_direction']=='positive']; neg=[r for r in rs if r['correlation_direction']=='negative']\n        pm=mean([r['oracle_global_worse_rate'] for r in pos]) if pos else float('nan')\n        nm=mean([r['oracle_global_worse_rate'] for r in neg]) if neg else float('nan')\n        om=mean([r['oracle_global_gate_rmse']-r['uncorrected_gate_rmse'] for r in rs])\n        print(key,'pos_worse',round(pm,4),'neg_worse',round(nm,4),'diff',round(pm-nm,4),'rmse_delta_mean',round(om,5),'score_range',(round(min(r['bias_bound_score'] for r in rs),6),round(max(r['bias_bound_score'] for r in rs),6)))\n# matched pair deltas\npairs=[]\nfor mis,cate,k,n,b in itertools.product(['low','high'],['low','high'],[3,6],[2000,5000,10000],[100,500,1000,2000]):\n    pos=[r for r in rows if r['misclassification_heterogeneity']==mis and r['cate_heterogeneity']==cate and r['k']==k and r['n']==n and r['expert_budget']==b and r['correlation_direction']=='positive'][0]\n    neg=[r for r in rows if r['misclassification_heterogeneity']==mis and r['cate_heterogeneity']==cate and r['k']==k and r['n']==n and r['expert_budget']==b and r['correlation_direction']=='negative'][0]\n    pairs.append((pos['oracle_global_worse_rate']-neg['oracle_global_worse_rate'], mis,cate,k,n,b,pos,neg))\nprint('\\\\nmatched pair deltas summary')\nvals=[x[0] for x in pairs]\nprint('n',len(vals),'min',min(vals),'max',max(vals),'mean',mean(vals),'positive_count',sum(v>0 for v in vals),'zero_count',sum(abs(v)<1e-9 for v in vals),'negative_count',sum(v<0 for v in vals))\nprint('top positive deltas')\nfor v,mis,cate,k,n,b,pos,neg in sorted(pairs, reverse=True)[:10]:\n    print(round(v,3),mis,cate,'k',k,'N',n,'B',b,'pos_rate',round(pos['oracle_global_worse_rate'],3),'neg_rate',round(neg['oracle_global_worse_rate'],3),'pos_rmse_delta',round(pos['oracle_global_gate_rmse']-pos['uncorrected_gate_rmse'],5),'neg_rmse_delta',round(neg['oracle_global_gate_rmse']-neg['uncorrected_gate_rmse'],5),'score',round(pos['bias_bound_score'],6))\nprint('top negative deltas')\nfor v,mis,cate,k,n,b,pos,neg in sorted(pairs)[:10]:\n    print(round(v,3),mis,cate,'k',k,'N',n,'B',b,'pos_rate',round(pos['oracle_global_worse_rate'],3),'neg_rate',round(neg['oracle_global_worse_rate'],3),'pos_rmse_delta',round(pos['oracle_global_gate_rmse']-pos['uncorrected_gate_rmse'],5),'neg_rmse_delta',round(neg['oracle_global_gate_rmse']-neg['uncorrected_gate_rmse'],5),'score',round(pos['bias_bound_score'],6))\n# conditions where oracle global mean rmse worse than uncorrected\nworse=[r for r in rows if r['oracle_global_gate_rmse']>r['uncorrected_gate_rmse']]\nbetter=[r for r in rows if r['oracle_global_gate_rmse']<=r['uncorrected_gate_rmse']]\nprint('\\\\nmean RMSE worse conditions',len(worse),'better/equal',len(better))\nfor name,subset in [('worse',worse),('better',better)]:\n    print(name,'worse_rate_range', (min(r['oracle_global_worse_rate'] for r in subset), max(r['oracle_global_worse_rate'] for r in subset)) if subset else None,'bias_score_range',(min(r['bias_bound_score'] for r in subset),max(r['bias_bound_score'] for r in subset)) if subset else None)\n    for dims in [('misclassification_heterogeneity','cate_heterogeneity'),('k',),('n',),('correlation_direction',)]:\n        counts={}\n        for r in subset:\n            key=tuple(r[d] for d in dims)\n            counts[key]=counts.get(key,0)+1\n        print(' ',dims,counts)\n# stratified vs uncorrected/global\nprint('\\\\nstratified summary')\nfor dims in [('misclassification_heterogeneity','cate_heterogeneity'),('k',)]:\n    groups={}\n    for r in rows:\n        groups.setdefault(tuple(r[d] for d in dims),[]).append(r)\n    print('dims',dims)\n    for k0,rs in sorted(groups.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0])):\n        print(k0,'strat_minus_unc',round(mean([r['stratified_subgroup_gate_rmse']-r['uncorrected_gate_rmse'] for r in rs]),5),'strat_minus_global',round(mean([r['stratified_subgroup_gate_rmse']-r['oracle_global_gate_rmse'] for r in rs]),5))\nPY\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}

更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\\\\n\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\n\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\n\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\n\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\n\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\n\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\n\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\n\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\n\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` 与 `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse`；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_002：positive-correlation conditions 的 oracle-global-worse rate 至少比 negative-correlation controls 高 20 percentage points，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0；bias-bound diagnostic 对 oracle-global-worse conditions 的 Spearman rho >= 0.50，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。estimated-global 结果必须与 oracle-global 分开报告。\\\\n\\\\nH2 success for Stage 1：low expert-label budget 下，partial pooling 在 GATE RMSE 上优于 global correction，`partial_pooling_minus_global` bootstrap 95% CI upper bound < 0；相对 per-subgroup independent correction 满足 5% relative GATE RMSE non-inferiority。PEHE 和 mean subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics。\\\\n\\\\n## 短期计划\\\\n\\\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\\\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\n4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\\\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup variables、可用 split 与复现实验权限；未确认前不把 real-data 写成已完成结果。\\\\n\\\\n## Experiment Plan / Matrix 映射\\\\n\\\\n当前最低覆盖目标：\\\\n- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\\\\n- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\\\\n- `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation` → `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`, `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`。\\\\n- `plan_004_realdata_feasibility` → `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY`。\\\\n\\\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\\\n\\\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\\\n- oracle global correction vs estimated global correction vs uncorrected\\\\n\\\\nStage 1 correction：\\\\n- uncorrected\\\\n- global confusion correction\\\\n- arm-specific correction\\\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\\\n\\\\nStage 2 inference：\\\\n- naive CI\\\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\\\n- bootstrap CI\\\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\\\n\\\\nStage 3 allocation：\\\\n- uniform allocation\\\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\\\n- joint criterion\\\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\\\n\\\\nReal-data validation：\\\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\n\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\n\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\n\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\n\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra、oracle-vs-estimated 拆分和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\\n\\\\n## 关键决策日志\\\\n\\\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\\\n| D002 | 2026-05-31 | exp002 grid does not support H1 grid-level success | exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid, exp_001_bias_bound_mvp |\\\\n| D001 | 2026-05-31 | H1 CPU MVP supports global-correction-worse mechan | exp_001_bias_bound_mvp, exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid |\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 5724,
