true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Memory\\\\n\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\n\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\n\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\n\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\\\n\\\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\\\n\\\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\\\\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\\\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\\\n\\\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\\\\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\\\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## 首批实验协议\\\\n\\\\n### 统一评测输出\\\\n\\\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\\\n\\\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\\\n\\\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\\\n\\\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\\\n\\\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\\\\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\\\\\"。\\\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## Real-data 可行性\\\\n\\\\n- Rohingya corpus 判定：**部分可支撑**。Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao 2025 的 Rohingya/Bengali host interview corpus 足以支撑 real-data validation 设计与 differential LLM misclassification 论证，但公开材料不足以直接复现实验；需要向作者/World Bank 申请 anonymized transcripts、record-level expert labels、record-level LLM labels 与 split IDs。来源：SAGE DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；arXiv DOI https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2309.17147；代码库 https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- 数据可得性与文本：论文报告 2,407 个 open-ended interviews、32,463 个 question-answer pairs；访谈在 Bengali/Rohingya dialect 中进行，转写为 Bengali，再经 Google Translate 翻译成 English 并人工校正后编码。Data Availability Statement 明确 code public，transcripts 因 privacy 不能公开下载，anonymised version will be available upon request。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246。\\\\n- Schema 与 labels：coding tree 包括 aspiration、ambition、navigational capacity 等层级，论文主分析聚焦 19 个 binary codes；789 transcripts 由两名 annotators 在 Atlas.ti 中 human-coded，并经 reviewer/weekly resolution process 处理分歧。LLM annotation 覆盖 GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4o mini、Llama-2 13B、Llama-3 8B，使用 code-specific instructions、few-shot prompting 和 chain-of-thought prompting；public repo 未发现 record-level Rohingya labels/LLM outputs。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；repo tree https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- Subgroup variables：refugee status、eldest child's gender、parent's years of education 被论文显式用于 Figure 8/9 regressions；quantitative table 还列出 female household head、household head age、number of children、parent religious education 等变量。World Bank summary 也明确 LLM errors are not random with respect to refugee status, gender, education。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；World Bank newsletter https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf。\\\\n- Split 与 license/IRB：paper 描述 R2/R3 sampling、expert-coded sample、iQual 80/20 test split 与 3-fold CV，但公开 split IDs 待核验；iQual code 是 MIT license，CBPS baseline Dataverse DOI https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB metadata reports CC0 1.0，World Bank HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33 是 public-use files。Transcript license、data-use agreement、IRB/consent constraints 待核验。来源：https://github.com/worldbank/iQual/blob/main/LICENSE.md；https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB；https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33。\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/fields_and_label_sources/data_schema_summary/exp_id=null/source=partial_support: public paper documents 2,407 interviews, 32,463 QA pairs, binary QA/interview code labels, 789 expert-coded transcripts, and LLM annotation methods; raw transcripts and record-level labels require request. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; repo https://github.com/worldbank/iQual`\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/expert_split_available/expert_split_status/exp_id=null/source=partial_support_pending_access: expert-coded sample and train/test/CV design are described, but public split IDs and record-level labels were not found. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246`\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/subgroup_variables_available/subgroup_variable_coverage/exp_id=null/source=support_for_variables_pending_linkage: refugee status, eldest-child gender, and parent education are explicitly used in the paper; public CBPS survey metadata supports host/camp and household/individual variables, but transcript-label linkage requires restricted/anonymized data access. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; CBPS https://macmillan.yale.edu/refugee/coxs-bazar-panel-survey; HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33`\\\\n\\\\n## 执行事件记录\\\\n\\\\n- [2026-05-31] \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Memory\\\\n\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\n\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\n\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\n\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\\\n\\\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\\\n\\\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\\\\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\\\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\\\n\\\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\\\\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\\\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## 首批实验协议\\\\n\\\\n### 统一评测输出\\\\n\\\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\\\n\\\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\\\n\\\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\\\n\\\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\\\n\\\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\\\\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\\\\\"。\\\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## Real-data 可行性\\\\n\\\\n- Rohingya corpus 判定：**部分可支撑**。Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao 2025 的 Rohingya/Bengali host interview corpus 足以支撑 real-data validation 设计与 differential LLM misclassification 论证，但公开材料不足以直接复现实验；需要向作者/World Bank 申请 anonymized transcripts、record-level expert labels、record-level LLM labels 与 split IDs。来源：SAGE DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；arXiv DOI https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2309.17147；代码库 https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- 数据可得性与文本：论文报告 2,407 个 open-ended interviews、32,463 个 question-answer pairs；访谈在 Bengali/Rohingya dialect 中进行，转写为 Bengali，再经 Google Translate 翻译成 English 并人工校正后编码。Data Availability Statement 明确 code public，transcripts 因 privacy 不能公开下载，anonymised version will be available upon request。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246。\\\\n- Schema 与 labels：coding tree 包括 aspiration、ambition、navigational capacity 等层级，论文主分析聚焦 19 个 binary codes；789 transcripts 由两名 annotators 在 Atlas.ti 中 human-coded，并经 reviewer/weekly resolution process 处理分歧。LLM annotation 覆盖 GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4o mini、Llama-2 13B、Llama-3 8B，使用 code-specific instructions、few-shot prompting 和 chain-of-thought prompting；public repo 未发现 record-level Rohingya labels/LLM outputs。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；repo tree https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- Subgroup variables：refugee status、eldest child's gender、parent's years of education 被论文显式用于 Figure 8/9 regressions；quantitative table 还列出 female household head、household head age、number of children、parent religious education 等变量。World Bank summary 也明确 LLM errors are not random with respect to refugee status, gender, education。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；World Bank newsletter https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf。\\\\n- Split 与 license/IRB：paper 描述 R2/R3 sampling、expert-coded sample、iQual 80/20 test split 与 3-fold CV，但公开 split IDs 待核验；iQual code 是 MIT license，CBPS baseline Dataverse DOI https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB metadata reports CC0 1.0，World Bank HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33 是 public-use files。Transcript license、data-use agreement、IRB/consent constraints 待核验。来源：https://github.com/worldbank/iQual/blob/main/LICENSE.md；https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB；https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33。\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/fields_and_label_sources/data_schema_summary/exp_id=null/source=partial_support: public paper documents 2,407 interviews, 32,463 QA pairs, binary QA/interview code labels, 789 expert-coded transcripts, and LLM annotation methods; raw transcripts and record-level labels require request. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; repo https://github.com/worldbank/iQual`\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/expert_split_available/expert_split_status/exp_id=null/source=partial_support_pending_access: expert-coded sample and train/test/CV design are described, but public split IDs and record-level labels were not found. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246`\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/subgroup_variables_available/subgroup_variable_coverage/exp_id=null/source=support_for_variables_pending_linkage: refugee status, eldest-child gender, and parent education are explicitly used in the paper; public CBPS survey metadata supports host/camp and household/individual variables, but transcript-label linkage requires restricted/anonymized data access. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; CBPS https://macmillan.yale.edu/refugee/coxs-bazar-panel-survey; HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33`\\\\n\\\\n## 执行事件记录\\\\n\\\\n- [2026-05-31] observed_gate(y: torch.Tensor, treatment: torch.Tensor, labels: torch.Tensor, k: int) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    estimates = []\\n    for group in range(k):\\n        mask = labels == group\\n        treated = mask & (treatment == 1)\\n        control = mask & (treatment == 0)\\n        if treated.any() and control.any():\\n            estimates.append(y[treated].mean() - y[control].mean())\\n        else:\\n            estimates.append(torch.tensor(float(\\\"nan\\\"), device=y.device))\\n    out = torch.stack(estimates)\\n    if torch.isnan(out).any():\\n        global_ate = y[treatment == 1].mean() - y[treatment == 0].mean()\\n        out = torch.where(torch.isnan(out), global_ate, out)\\n    return out\\n\\n\\ndef correct_gate(observed: torch.Tensor, transition: torch.Tensor, ridge: float) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    k = transition.shape[0]\\n    lhs = transition.T + ridge * torch.eye(k, device=transition.device)\\n    return torch.linalg.solve(lhs, observed)\\n\\n\\ndef gate_rmse(estimate: torch.Tensor, truth: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    return torch.sqrt(torch.mean((estimate - truth) ** 2))\\n\\n\\ndef bias_bound_score(rates: torch.Tensor, cate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    centered_rate = rates - rates.mean()\\n    centered_cate = torch.abs(cate) - torch.abs(cate).mean()\\n    return torch.mean(torch.abs(centered_rate * centered_cate))\\n\\n\\ndef rankdata(values: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    order = torch.argsort(values)\\n    ranks = torch.empty_like(values, dtype=torch.float32)\\n    ranks[order] = torch.arange(values.numel(), device=values.device, dtype=torch.float32)\\n    return ranks\\n\\n\\ndef spearman(x: torch.Tensor, y: torch.Tensor) -> float:\\n    if x.numel() < 2 or torch.std(x) == 0 or torch.std(y) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    rx = rankdata(x.float())\\n    ry = rankdata(y.float())\\n    rx = rx - rx.mean()\\n    ry = ry - ry.mean()\\n    denom = torch.sqrt(torch.sum(rx**2) * torch.sum(ry**2))\\n    if denom == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return float(torch.sum(rx * ry) / denom)\\n\\n\\ndef quantile(values: list[float], q: float) -> float:\\n    if not values:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    sorted_values = sorted(values)\\n    idx = min(max(int(math.floor(q * (len(sorted_values) - 1))), 0), len(sorted_values) - 1)\\n    return float(sorted_values[idx])\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_metrics(\\n    condition_rows: list[dict[str, Any]],\\n    iterations: int,\\n    seed: int,\\n    device: torch.device,\\n) -> dict[str, float]:\\n    pos = [r for r in condition_rows if r[\\\"correlation_direction\\\"] == \\\"positive\\\"]\\n    neg = [r for r in condition_rows if r[\\\"correlation_direction\\\"] == \\\"negative\\\"]\\n    if not pos or not neg:\\n        return {\\n            \\\"primary_ci_lower\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n            \\\"primary_ci_upper\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n            \\\"spearman_ci_lower\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n            \\\"spearman_ci_upper\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n        }\\n\\n    generator = torch.Generator(device=device)\\n    generator.manual_seed(seed)\\n    primary_samples: list[float] = []\\n    spearman_samples: list[float] = []\\n    rows = condition_rows\\n    for _ in range(iterations):\\n        pos_idx = torch.randint(0, len(pos), (len(pos),), generator=generator, device=device)\\n        neg_idx = torch.randint(0, len(neg), (len(neg),), generator=generator, device=device)\\n        row_idx = torch.randint(0, len(rows), (len(rows),), generator=generator, device=device)\\n        pos_rate = torch.tensor(\\n            [pos[int(i)][\\\"oracle_global_worse_rate\\\"] for i in pos_idx],\\n            device=device,\\n        ).mean()\\n        neg_rate = torch.tensor(\\n            [neg[int(i)][\\\"oracle_global_worse_rate\\\"] for i in neg_idx],\\n            device=device,\\n        ).mean()\\n        primary_samples.append(float(pos_rate - neg_rate))\\n        x = torch.tensor([rows[int(i)][\\\"bias_bound_score\\\"] for i in row_idx], device=device)\\n        y = torch.tensor([rows[int(i)][\\\"oracle_global_worse_rate\\\"] for i in row_idx], device=device)\\n        rho = spearman(x, y)\\n        if not math.isnan(rho):\\n            spearman_samples.append(rho)\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"primary_ci_lower\\\": quantile(primary_samples, 0.025),\\n        \\\"primary_ci_upper\\\": quantile(primary_samples, 0.975),\\n        \\\"spearman_ci_lower\\\": quantile(spearman_samples, 0.025),\\n        \\\"spearman_ci_upper\\\": quantile(spearman_samples, 0.975),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef run_condition(\\n    condition: Condition,\\n    replications: int,\\n    config: dict[str, Any],\\n    device: torch.device,\\n    seed: int,\\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\n    k = condition.k\\n    n = condition.n\\n    budget = min(condition.expert_budget, n)\\n    ridge = float(config[\\\"global_correction_ridge\\\"])\\n    cate = cate_values(k, condition.cate_heterogeneity, device)\\n    rates = error_rates(\\n        k,\\n        condition.misclassification_heterogeneity,\\n        cate,\\n        condition.correlation_direction,\\n        device,\\n    )\\n    subgroup_transition = transition_matrix_from_rates(rates)\\n    oracle_global_transition = global_transition_matrix(rates.mean(), k, device)\\n    true_gate = cate\\n    metrics = {\\n        \\\"uncorrected_gate_rmse\\\": [],\\n        \\\"oracle_global_gate_rmse\\\": [],\\n        \\\"estimated_global_gate_rmse\\\": [],\\n        \\\"stratified_subgroup_gate_rmse\\\": [],\\n        \\\"oracle_global_worse\\\": [],\\n        \\\"estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse\\\": [],\\n    }\\n\\n    for _ in range(replications):\\n        z_true = torch.randint(0, k, (n,), device=device)\\n        z_noisy = sample_noisy_labels(z_true, rates)\\n        treatment = (\\n            torch.rand(n, device=device) < float(config[\\\"treatment_probability\\\"])\\n        ).long()\\n        y = (\\n            0.2\\n            + true_gate[z_true] * treatment.float()\\n            + torch.randn(n, device=device) * float(config[\\\"noise_sd\\\"])\\n        )\\n        obs_gate = observed_gate(y, treatment, z_noisy, k)\\n        uncorrected = obs_gate\\n        oracle_global = correct_gate(obs_gate, oracle_global_transition, ridge)\\n\\n        validation_idx = torch.randperm(n, device=device)[:budget]\\n        estimated_error = (z_true[validation_idx] != z_noisy[validation_idx]).float().mean()\\n        estimated_global_transition = global_transition_matrix(estimated_error, k, device)\\n        estimated_global = correct_gate(obs_gate, estimated_global_transition, ridge)\\n        stratified_subgroup = correct_gate(obs_gate, subgroup_transition, ridge)\\n\\n        uncorrected_rmse = gate_rmse(uncorrected, true_gate)\\n        oracle_global_rmse = gate_rmse(oracle_global, true_gate)\\n        estimated_global_rmse = gate_rmse(estimated_global, true_gate)\\n        stratified_rmse = gate_rmse(stratified_subgroup, true_gate)\\n\\n        metrics[\\\"uncorrected_gate_rmse\\\"].append(uncorrected_rmse)\\n        metrics[\\\"oracle_global_gate_rmse\\\"].append(oracle_global_rmse)\\n        metrics[\\\"estimated_global_gate_rmse\\\"].append(estimated_global_rmse)\\n        metrics[\\\"stratified_subgroup_gate_rmse\\\"].append(stratified_rmse)\\n        metrics[\\\"oracle_global_worse\\\"].append((oracle_global_rmse > uncorrected_rmse).float())\\n        metrics[\\\"estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse\\\"].append(\\n            estimated_global_rmse - oracle_global_rmse\\n        )\\n\\n    reduced = {key: float(torch.stack(vals).mean()) for key, vals in metrics.items()}\\n    reduced[\\\"oracle_global_worse_rate\\\"] = reduced.pop(\\\"oracle_global_worse\\\")\\n    reduced.update(\\n        {\\n            \\\"condition_id\\\": condition.condition_id,\\n            \\\"correlation_direction\\\": condition.correlation_direction,\\n            \\\"misclassification_heterogeneity\\\": condition.misclassification_heterogeneity,\\n            \\\"cate_heterogeneity\\\": condition.cate_heterogeneity,\\n            \\\"k\\\": condition.k,\\n            \\\"n\\\": condition.n,\\n -lc \\\"sed -n '100,170p' repro/exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid/run_gpu_dgp_grid.py\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"\\ndef cate_values(k: int, heterogeneity: str, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    if heterogeneity == \\\"high\\\":\\n        vals = torch.linspace(0.15, 1.15, k, device=device)\\n    else:\\n        vals = torch.linspace(0.35, 0.65, k, device=device)\\n    return vals - vals.mean() + 0.5\\n\\n\\ndef error_rates(\\n    k: int,\\n    mis_heterogeneity: str,\\n    cate: torch.Tensor,\\n    direction: str,\\n    device: torch.device,\\n) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    if mis_heterogeneity == \\\"high\\\":\\n        low, high = 0.04, 0.36\\n    else:\\n        low, high = 0.10, 0.18\\n    ordered = torch.linspace(low, high, k, device=device)\\n    order = torch.argsort(torch.abs(cate), descending=False)\\n    rates = torch.empty(k, device=device)\\n    if direction == \\\"positive\\\":\\n        rates[order] = ordered\\n    else:\\n        rates[order] = torch.flip(ordered, dims=[0])\\n    return rates\\n\\n\\ndef transition_matrix_from_rates(rates: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    k = int(rates.numel())\\n    matrix = torch.zeros((k, k), device=rates.device)\\n    off_diag = rates / max(k - 1, 1)\\n    matrix[:] = off_diag[:, None]\\n    diag_idx = torch.arange(k, device=rates.device)\\n    matrix[diag_idx, diag_idx] = 1.0 - rates\\n    return matrix\\n\\n\\ndef global_transition_matrix(p_error: torch.Tensor, k: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    rates = torch.full((k,), p_error.clamp(0.0, 0.95), device=device)\\n    return transition_matrix_from_rates(rates)\\n\\n\\ndef sample_noisy_labels(z_true: torch.Tensor, rates: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    k = int(rates.numel())\\n    device = z_true.device\\n    error = torch.rand_like(z_true.float()) < rates[z_true]\\n    shift = torch.randint(1, k, z_true.shape, device=device)\\n    z_alt = (z_true + shift) % k\\n    return torch.where(error, z_alt, z_true)\\n\\n\\ndef observed_gate(y: torch.Tensor, treatment: torch.Tensor, labels: torch.Tensor, k: int) -> torch.Tensor:\\n    estimates = []\\n    for group in range(k):\\n        mask = labels == group\\n        treated = mask & (treatment == 1)\\n        control = mask & (treatment == 0)\\n        if treated.any() and control.any():\\n            estimates.append(y[treated].mean() - y[control].mean())\\n        else:\\n            estimates.append(torch.tensor(float(\\\"nan\\\"), device=y.device))\\n    out = torch.stack(estimates)\\n    if torch.isnan(out).any():\\n        global_ate = y[treatment == 1].mean() - y[treatment == 0].mean()\\n        out = torch.where(torch.isnan(out), global_ate, out)\\n    return out\\n\\n\\n\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}\n{\"type\":\"item.started\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_26\",\"type\":\"web_search\",\"id\":\"ws_0558368b63135724016a1c6d6109d88191b7566c2802bc2233\",\"query\":\"\",\"action\":{\"type\":\"other\"}}}\n{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_26\",\"type\":\"web_search\",\"id\":\"ws_0558368b63135724016a1c6d6109d88191b7566c2802bc2233\",\"query\":\"Kuchenhoff 更新原因: 量化 H1/exp_002 判据并新增 H2 partial-pooling claim\\\\n\\\\n## 项目目标\\\\n\\\\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\\\\n\\\\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\\\\n\\\\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 可能高于 uncorrected。\\\\n\\\\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\n\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\n\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\n\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\n\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\n\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` 与 `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse`；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。\\\\n\\\\nH1 success for exp_002：positive-correlation conditions 的 oracle-global-worse rate 至少比 negative-correlation controls 高 20 percentage points，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0；bias-bound diagnostic 对 oracle-global-worse conditions 的 Spearman rho >= 0.50，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。estimated-global 结果必须与 oracle-global 分开报告。\\\\n\\\\nH2 success for Stage 1：low expert-label budget 下，partial pooling 在 GATE RMSE 上优于 global correction，`partial_pooling_minus_global` bootstrap 95% CI upper bound < 0；相对 per-subgroup independent correction 满足 5% relative GATE RMSE non-inferiority。PEHE 和 mean subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics。\\\\n\\\\n## 短期计划\\\\n\\\\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1。必跑对照为 uncorrected、oracle global confusion correction、estimated global confusion correction、stratified/subgroup correction；必须拆开结构性 global-correction 偏差与 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\\\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\n4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\\\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup variables、可用 split 与复现实验权限；未确认前不把 real-data 写成已完成结果。\\\\n\\\\n## Experiment Plan / Matrix 映射\\\\n\\\\n当前最低覆盖目标：\\\\n- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\\\\n- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\\\\n- `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation` → `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`, `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`。\\\\n- `plan_004_realdata_feasibility` → `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY`。\\\\n\\\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\\\n\\\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\\\n- oracle global correction vs estimated global correction vs uncorrected\\\\n\\\\nStage 1 correction：\\\\n- uncorrected\\\\n- global confusion correction\\\\n- arm-specific correction\\\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\\\n\\\\nStage 2 inference：\\\\n- naive CI\\\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\\\n- bootstrap CI\\\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\\\n\\\\nStage 3 allocation：\\\\n- uniform allocation\\\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\\\n- joint criterion\\\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\\\n\\\\nReal-data validation：\\\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\n\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\n\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\n\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\n\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra、oracle-vs-estimated 拆分和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\\n\\\\n## 关键决策日志\\\\n\\\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\\\n| D002 | 2026-05-31 | exp002 grid does not support H1 grid-level success | exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid, exp_001_bias_bound_mvp |\\\\n| D001 | 2026-05-31 | H1 CPU MVP supports global-correction-worse mechan | exp_001_bias_bound_mvp, exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid |\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 5724, confusion-estimation noise。\\\\n2. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 只能在 PENDING 审批、QUEUED、Director GPU allocation 后启动。GPU 实验必须使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`，并在 spec 中记录 command、server、GPU 型号、driver、data_path/data_count、seed、disk estimate、expected runtime、log/checkpoint/artifact 路径。\\\\n3. 建立统一评测协议：GATE RMSE、subgroup CATE bias、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\\\\n4. 维护 `experiment_plan.yaml` 与 experiment matrix 映射：H1 claim、H2 claim、Stage 0 DGP grid、Stage 1 correction ablation、real-data feasibility 均必须有 plan entry 和 target/cell 映射。\\\\n5. 并行检查 real-data validation 可行性：Rohingya corpus 字段、LLM annotation、expert label source、subgroup variables、可用 split 与复现实验权限；未确认前不把 real-data 写成已完成结果。\\\\n\\\\n## Experiment Plan / Matrix 映射\\\\n\\\\n当前最低覆盖目标：\\\\n- `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`。\\\\n- `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` → `C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE`, `T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID`, registry `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`。\\\\n- `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation` → `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`, `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`。\\\\n- `plan_004_realdata_feasibility` → `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY`。\\\\n\\\\n## 中期实验矩阵\\\\n\\\\nStage 0 机制验证：\\\\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\\\\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\\\\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\\\\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\\\\n- oracle global correction vs estimated global correction vs uncorrected\\\\n\\\\nStage 1 correction：\\\\n- uncorrected\\\\n- global confusion correction\\\\n- arm-specific correction\\\\n- per-subgroup independent correction\\\\n- hierarchical/partial-pooling correction\\\\n\\\\nStage 2 inference：\\\\n- naive CI\\\\n- sandwich-adjusted CI\\\\n- bootstrap CI\\\\n- empirical coverage 与 CI width 对比\\\\n\\\\nStage 3 allocation：\\\\n- uniform allocation\\\\n- misclassification-uncertainty-only\\\\n- CATE-magnitude-only\\\\n- joint criterion\\\\n- pilot ratio 10%、20%、30%\\\\n\\\\nReal-data validation：\\\\n- Rohingya interview corpus 上验证 LLM misclassification 是否随 refugee status、gender、education 差异化变化\\\\n- leave-expert-out calibration test\\\\n- diagnostic criterion operability demonstration\\\\n\\\\n## 工程产出\\\\n\\\\n- `scripts/`：可复现实验入口，包含 DGP、correction、estimation、evaluation。\\\\n- `configs/`：每个实验的 YAML/JSON 配置，记录 seed、DGP 参数、预算、method。\\\\n- `artifacts/`：实验表格、图、日志、registry 可追溯输出。\\\\n- `docs/`：技术报告素材与复现说明，由 Director 汇总。\\\\n- 部署代码：提供 diagnostic calculator 和 correction workflow 的最小可调用接口。\\\\n\\\\n## 决策规则\\\\n\\\\n- 任何负向结果必须先汇报 Director，不得默默跑同一方向的下一步。\\\\n- 如果 exp_001 未能复现 global correction worse than uncorrected，优先诊断 DGP、correction algebra、oracle-vs-estimated 拆分和 metric 定义，而不是直接推进 Stage 1。\\\\n- GPU 实验启动前必须确认项目白名单 GPU 可见，并记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径、seed。\\\\n- 所有定量结论只引用 registry 中有 exp_id 的结果。\\\\n\\\\n## 关键决策日志\\\\n\\\\n| ID | 日期 | 摘要 | 关联实验 |\\\\n|----|------|------|----------|\\\\n| D002 | 2026-05-31 | exp002 grid does not support H1 grid-level success | exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid, exp_001_bias_bound_mvp |\\\\n| D001 | 2026-05-31 | H1 CPU MVP supports global-correction-worse mechan | exp_001_bias_bound_mvp, exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid |\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 5724, true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# Memory\\\\n\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\n\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\n\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\n\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\\\n\\\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\\\n\\\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\\\\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\\\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\\\n\\\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\\\\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\\\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## 首批实验协议\\\\n\\\\n### 统一评测输出\\\\n\\\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\\\n\\\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\\\n\\\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\\\n\\\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\\\n\\\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\\\\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\\\\\"。\\\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## Real-data 可行性\\\\n\\\\n- Rohingya corpus 判定：**部分可支撑**。Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao 2025 的 Rohingya/Bengali host interview corpus 足以支撑 real-data validation 设计与 differential LLM misclassification 论证，但公开材料不足以直接复现实验；需要向作者/World Bank 申请 anonymized transcripts、record-level expert labels、record-level LLM labels 与 split IDs。来源：SAGE DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；arXiv DOI https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2309.17147；代码库 https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- 数据可得性与文本：论文报告 2,407 个 open-ended interviews、32,463 个 question-answer pairs；访谈在 Bengali/Rohingya dialect 中进行，转写为 Bengali，再经 Google Translate 翻译成 English 并人工校正后编码。Data Availability Statement 明确 code public，transcripts 因 privacy 不能公开下载，anonymised version will be available upon request。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246。\\\\n- Schema 与 labels：coding tree 包括 aspiration、ambition、navigational capacity 等层级，论文主分析聚焦 19 个 binary codes；789 transcripts 由两名 annotators 在 Atlas.ti 中 human-coded，并经 reviewer/weekly resolution process 处理分歧。LLM annotation 覆盖 GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4o mini、Llama-2 13B、Llama-3 8B，使用 code-specific instructions、few-shot prompting 和 chain-of-thought prompting；public repo 未发现 record-level Rohingya labels/LLM outputs。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；repo tree https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- Subgroup variables：refugee status、eldest child's gender、parent's years of education 被论文显式用于 Figure 8/9 regressions；quantitative table 还列出 female household head、household head age、number of children、parent religious education 等变量。World Bank summary 也明确 LLM errors are not random with respect to refugee status, gender, education。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；World Bank newsletter https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf。\\\\n- Split 与 license/IRB：paper 描述 R2/R3 sampling、expert-coded sample、iQual 80/20 test split 与 3-fold CV，但公开 split IDs 待核验；iQual code 是 MIT license，CBPS baseline Dataverse DOI https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB metadata reports CC0 1.0，World Bank HFT DOI https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33 是 public-use files。Transcript license、data-use agreement、IRB/consent constraints 待核验。来源：https://github.com/worldbank/iQual/blob/main/LICENSE.md；https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB；https://doi.org/10.48529/2zfw-ht33。\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/fields_and_label_sources/data_schema_summary/exp_id=null/source=partial_support: public paper documents 2,407 interviews, 32,463 QA pairs, binary QA/interview code labels, 789 expert-coded transcripts, and LLM annotation methods; raw transcripts and record-level labels require request. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246; repo https://github.com/worldbank/iQual`\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/expert_split_available/expert_split_status/exp_id=null/source=partial_support_pending_access: expert-coded sample and train/test/CV design are described, but public split IDs and record-level labels were not found. DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246`\\\\n- Matrix provenance: `C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY/rohingya_corpus/subgroup_variables_available/subgroup_variable_coverage/exp_id=null/source=support_for_variables_pending_linkage: refugee status, eldest-child gender, and parent education are explicitly used in the paper; public CBPS survey metadata supports host/camp and household/individual variables, but transcript-label linkage requires restricted/anonymized data access.
