\\\"content\\\": \\\"# Memory\\\\n\\\\n## 项目定位\\\\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\\\\n\\\\n## 执行约束\\\\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\\\\n\\\\n## 领域参考\\\\n\\\\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\\\\n\\\\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\\\\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\\\\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \\\\\\\"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\\\\\\\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\\\\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat_i - tau_i)^2))`，仅用于 synthetic/semi-synthetic 中 ground-truth tau_i 已知的设置。项目 brief 已列 PEHE 为主指标。\\\\n- GATE: Group Average Treatment Effect 是预先定义 subgroup 内的平均处理效应，DoubleML 文档定义为 `E[Y(1)-Y(0)|G=1]`（https://docs.doubleml.org/tutorial/stable/lectures/L3/heterogeneity.html）；Stata 19 CATE 文档也把 GATE/GATES 作为 IATE/CATE 的 group-level summaries（https://www.stata.com/stata19/cate/）。本项目 GATE RMSE 定义为 `sqrt(mean_g((tau_hat_g - tau_g)^2))`，用于 k 个真实 subgroup CATE 已知的 simulation。\\\\n- Replication / split reporting: 项目 brief 明确半合成实验使用 `R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、expert budget `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、subgroup count `k in {3, 6}`、treatment assignment `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。这些是首批实验的默认规模来源；任何缩小版只能标为 smoke/MVP，不写成正式结论。\\\\n\\\\n### LLM annotation debiasing 与适用边界\\\\n\\\\n- PPI: Angelopoulos, Bates, Fannjiang, Jordan & Zrnic, Science 2023 / arXiv:2301.09633（https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.09633；Science DOI https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adi6000）将少量 gold labels 与大量 ML predictions 结合，为 means、quantiles、linear/logistic regression coefficients 等 population parameters 构造有效 CI。本项目可把 PPI 作为 ATE / population regression coefficient correction baseline；不能直接声称它修正 misclassified categorical effect modifier 下的 subgroup CATE，除非把每个 subgroup estimand 单独建模并处理 subgroup membership error。\\\\n- DSL: Egami, Hinck, Stewart & Wei, NeurIPS 2023 / arXiv:2306.04746（https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.04746）针对 CSS 中 LLM/surrogate labels 的 downstream statistical analysis，使用 gold-standard labels 与 controlled sampling probability 来获得 asymptotic unbiasedness 和 uncertainty quantification。适合作为 downstream regression/ATE-style estimand 的 debiasing baseline；不自动解决 effect modifier 本身被错分导致的 CATE conditioning error。\\\\n- DSL/PPI finite-sample benchmark: Audinet de Pieuchon et al., EMNLP 2025（ACL Anthology https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1000/；arXiv:2506.09627）比较 DSL 与 PPI 在有限 expert labels 下的 LLM-based parameter estimate debiasing；结论范围是 population parameters / regression coefficients / causal effects 的 debiasing，不是 CATE modifier misclassification correction。可引用为为什么首批实验需要固定 expert budget curves。\\\\n- StratPPI: Fisch et al., NeurIPS 2024（https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/c9fcd02e6445c7dfbad6986abee53d0d-Abstract-Conference.html；arXiv:2406.04291）通过 stratified sampling 缩窄 PPI CI，尤其当 autorater performance across conditional distributions varies。EC-HTE 可把 StratPPI 作为 ATE/population mean 的 stratified correction baseline，或作为 expert allocation 思路来源；不能把它当成 categorical modifier confusion-matrix correction，除非 correction estimand 明确改成每个 subgroup 的 gold-label estimand。\\\\n- Active Statistical Inference / AM-PPI: Zrnic & Candès, ICML 2024 Active Statistical Inference（https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.03208；ICML page https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34504）用 model uncertainty 主动选择 gold labels 并构造有效 CI；Brawand et al., AM-PPI arXiv:2605.08429（https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08429）扩展为多 predictor、per-instance routing、residual uncertainty sampling。可作为 Stage 3 allocation baseline/灵感；其原始目标是 population mean monitoring/valid CI，不直接修 CATE conditioning variable 的 misclassification。\\\\n- Mozer difference-estimator view: Mozer, arXiv:2603.19160（https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19160）指出 PPI mean estimator 等价于 survey sampling difference estimator，并讨论 differential prediction error for subgroup estimands such as ATE。项目中的 \\\\\\\"arm-specific rectifier\\\\\\\" baseline 应仅实现为 treatment/control arm 内各自估计 LLM-vs-expert residual/confusion correction，然后估计 ATE/GATE；它仍然是 arm-level/population-level rectification，不能代替 subgroup-specific modifier confusion correction。\\\\n- Ashwin real-data motivation: Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao, Sociological Methods & Research 2025, DOI 10.1177/00491241251338246（publisher page https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/00491241251338246；Maastricht record https://cris.maastrichtuniversity.nl/en/publications/using-large-language-models-for-qualitative-analysis-can-introduc-2/）用 Rohingya/Bengali host interviews 说明 LLM coding errors are not random with respect to respondent characteristics。World Bank summary explicitly names refugee status, gender, education as non-random error correlates（https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf）。本项目 real-data validation 只用于验证 differential misclassification pattern，不用于声称真实 CATE accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n### Measurement error / categorical misclassification correction baselines\\\\n\\\\n- Global confusion matrix correction: 从 expert validation subset 估计单一 `P(Z_hat | Z)` 或 inverse matrix，把所有 subgroup 共用同一 misclassification mechanism。来源对应 epidemiology 中 matrix / inverse matrix correction for misclassification；见 Lyles et al. extended matrix/inverse matrix method with internal validation data（PMC https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4382468/）。本项目必须报告当 subgroup-specific error differs 时 global correction 的 under/over-correction。\\\\n- Per-subgroup confusion matrix correction: 在每个 true or pilot-estimated subgroup g 内估计 `C_g = P(Z_hat | Z, g)`，再做 correction。该 baseline 是 EC-HTE 的 non-pooled comparator；高方差风险来自每组 expert labels 少。来源是同一 validation-data misclassification framework；partial pooling 是本项目新增稳定化层，不能把 independent per-subgroup correction 写成已有 EC-HTE 结果。\\\\n- Arm-specific correction: treatment/control arm 内分别估计 confusion/residual correction，模拟 Mozer/PPI arm-level rectifier 对 ATE 的处理。适用 ATE 或 arm-specific mean outcome/label correction；不能直接修正 \\\\\\\"which subgroup is the unit conditioned on\\\\\\\" 的 modifier misclassification，除非同时对 subgroup-specific `C_{g,t}` 做建模。\\\\n- SIMEX categorical approximation: Kuchenhoff, Mwalili & Lesaffre, Biometrics 2006（PubMed https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/16542233/）把 SIMEX 扩展到 discrete covariate/response misclassification，通过 transition matrix Pi 增加误分类并外推到无误分类。EC-HTE 可实现为 categorical transition-matrix SIMEX + causal forest baseline；必须标记为近似 baseline，因为项目目标变量是 categorical effect modifier，而 classic SIMEX 更自然地服务 regression parameter correction。\\\\n- Calibration/probability modifier baseline: 用 expert validation subset 做 Platt scaling / temperature scaling 或 Dirichlet/multinomial calibration，把 LLM hard label 改成 calibrated class probabilities，再作为 soft modifier features 输入 CATE estimator。该实现只校准 predicted probabilities；如果 downstream CATE 仍按 hard subgroup 分层，它不会消除 subgroup membership error。概率校准作为一般 ML practice 可参考 Niculescu-Mizil & Caruana, ICML 2005（https://dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/1102351.1102430），但 EC-HTE 需在 artifact 中记录 calibration loss 和 subgroup confusion recovery，而非只报 predictive accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## 首批实验协议\\\\n\\\\n### 统一评测输出\\\\n\\\\n- 每个 experiment/config/replication 输出：seed、N、k、B、misclassification severity、CATE values、misclassification-CATE correlation direction、method、PEHE、per-subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、95% CI empirical coverage、mean/median CI width、bound diagnostic product、runtime、artifact path。指标来自项目 brief；PEHE/GATE 定义见上方领域参考。\\\\n- 正式 simulation 默认：`R >= 200` replications、`N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`、`B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`、`k in {3, 6}`、`T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`。来源：PROJECT_BRIEF.md Validation Design。\\\\n- Smoke/MVP 可以只跑 `N=2000`、`k=3`、少量 replications，但必须标注为 smoke/MVP，不得进入定量结论。来源：项目内部工程约束；正式规模仍以 brief 为准。\\\\n\\\\n### 实验矩阵建议\\\\n\\\\n| 实验 | 目标 | Metrics | Baselines | 最小样本规模 | 预计运行成本 |\\\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\\\n| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | 验证 positive misclassification-CATE correlation 下 global correction 可 worse than uncorrected | per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, bound diagnostic product; optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion matrix, oracle/stratified subgroup correction | 正式最小 `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200`; smoke 可低于此但仅作工程检查。来源：brief `N`/`k`/`R` | CPU-only 低成本；GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 1 partial pooling | 验证 hierarchical/partial-pooling confusion matrices 在小 B 下优于 global 与 per-subgroup independent | confusion recovery error, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup CATE bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent/no pooling, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle confusion | `N in {2000,5000}` 起步，`B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中等；VI 可本地跑，MCMC 只做 appendix representative configs。来源：brief Stage 1 VI/MCMC 描述 |\\\\n| Stage 2 CI | 验证 Stage 1 nuisance uncertainty 未传播时 CI under-cover；比较 sandwich/boot/naive | 95% empirical coverage, CI width, GATE RMSE, PEHE | naive causal forest CI, sandwich-adjusted CI, bootstrap CI | `N in {2000,5000,10000}`, `B in {500,1000,2000}`, `R>=200`。来源：brief | CPU 中到高；bootstrap 是主要成本来源，GPU 非必要 |\\\\n| Stage 3 allocation | 比较 adaptive expert annotation allocation 是否优于 uniform | PEHE at fixed B, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, confusion uncertainty, annotation allocation share | uniform, misclassification-uncertainty-only, CATE-magnitude-only, joint criterion, pilot ratios 10/20/30% | `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, pilot ratios `10%,20%,30%`, `R>=200`。来源：brief Stage 3 ablation | CPU 中等；多 budget/pilot sweep 带来配置数成本 |\\\\n| Real-data misclassification validation | 在 Rohingya interview corpus 验证 differential misclassification，并测试 leave-expert-out confusion recovery | subgroup error-rate gaps, confusion recovery error, calibration loss, budget curve; 不报真实 CATE accuracy | global confusion, per-subgroup independent, partial pooling, calibration/probability modifier | expert-label subsampling budget 复用 `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`；subgroups: refugee status/gender/education。来源：brief + Ashwin et al. 2025 / World Bank summary | 数据访问/清洗成本中等；不需要 GPU；若 transcripts 不可公开，artifact 只记录 aggregate metrics |\\\\n\\\\n### Baseline 实现规范\\\\n\\\\n- 所有 correction methods 使用同一 expert label budget B、同一 validation split、同一 random seed list；PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI 类方法只作为 ATE/population-parameter 或 allocation baselines，若用于 CATE 必须明确每个 subgroup estimand 的定义与 membership-error 处理。\\\\n- Global/per-subgroup/SIMEX/calibration baselines 都必须输出 estimated confusion matrix 或 transition matrix；没有 matrix recovery 输出的 run 不用于判断 EC-HTE 机制。\\\\n- Arm-specific rectifier 只能解释 arm-level residual correction 效果；如果它在 GATE/CATE 上失败，不写成 PPI/Mozer 失效的泛化结论，只写成 \\\\\\\"arm-level correction does not address modifier misclassification in this DGP\\\\\\\"。\\\\n- Real-data Rohingya validation 的结论边界：只验证 LLM coding errors 是否随 respondent characteristics 差异化、以及 partial pooling 是否更好恢复 subgroup-specific error patterns；由于缺少自然 causal ground truth，不报告 PEHE/GATE RMSE 作为真实 causal accuracy。\\\\n\\\\n## Real-data 可行性\\\\n\\\\n- Rohingya corpus 判定：**部分可支撑**。Ashwin, Chhabra & Rao 2025 的 Rohingya/Bengali host interview corpus 足以支撑 real-data validation 设计与 differential LLM misclassification 论证，但公开材料不足以直接复现实验；需要向作者/World Bank 申请 anonymized transcripts、record-level expert labels、record-level LLM labels 与 split IDs。来源：SAGE DOI https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；arXiv DOI https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2309.17147；代码库 https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- 数据可得性与文本：论文报告 2,407 个 open-ended interviews、32,463 个 question-answer pairs；访谈在 Bengali/Rohingya dialect 中进行，转写为 Bengali，再经 Google Translate 翻译成 English 并人工校正后编码。Data Availability Statement 明确 code public，transcripts 因 privacy 不能公开下载，anonymised version will be available upon request。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246。\\\\n- Schema 与 labels：coding tree 包括 aspiration、ambition、navigational capacity 等层级，论文主分析聚焦 19 个 binary codes；789 transcripts 由两名 annotators 在 Atlas.ti 中 human-coded，并经 reviewer/weekly resolution process 处理分歧。LLM annotation 覆盖 GPT-3.5-turbo、GPT-4o mini、Llama-2 13B、Llama-3 8B，使用 code-specific instructions、few-shot prompting 和 chain-of-thought prompting；public repo 未发现 record-level Rohingya labels/LLM outputs。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；repo tree https://github.com/worldbank/iQual。\\\\n- Subgroup variables：refugee status、eldest child's gender、parent's years of education 被论文显式用于 Figure 8/9 regressions；quantitative table 还列出 female household head、household head age、number of children、parent religious education 等变量。World Bank summary 也明确 LLM errors are not random with respect to refugee status, gender, education。来源：https://doi.org/10.1177/00491241251338246；World Bank newsletter https://documents1.worldbank.org/curated/en/099348304162431121/pdf/IDU1271bc2541c5e8144f51b8cc1be231f985cc6.pdf。\\\\n- Split 与 license/IRB：paper 描述 R2/R3 sampling、expert-coded sample、iQual 80/20 test split 与 3-fold CV，但公开 split IDs 待核验；iQual code 是 MIT license，CBPS baseline Dataverse DOI https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/V614VB metadata reports CC0 1.0，World Bank HFT DOI 22\\tEXP_ID = \\\"exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\\\"\\n    23\\t\\n    24\\t\\n    25\\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\\n    26\\tclass Condition:\\n    27\\t    condition_id: str\\n    28\\t    correlation_direction: parser.add_argument(\\\"--output-dir\\\", required=True)\\n    40\\t    parser.add_argument(\\\"--device\\\", default=\\\"cuda\\\", choices=[\\\"cuda\\\", \\\"cpu\\\"])\\n    41\\t    parser.add_argument(\\\"--max-replications\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    42\\t    parser.add_argument(\\\"--limit-conditions\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    43\\t    parser.add_argument(\\\"--dry-run\\\", action=\\\"store_true\\\")\\n    44\\t    return parser.parse_args()\\n    45\\t\\n    46\\t\\n    47\\tdef load_config(path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n    48\\t    with open(path, \\\"r\\\", encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\") as handle:\\n    49\\t        return json.load(handle)\\n    50\\t\\n    51\\t\\n    52\\tdef condition_grid(config: dict[str, Any]) -> list[Condition]:\\n    53\\t    conditions: list[Condition] = []\\n    54\\t    keys = [\\n    55\\t        config[\\\"misclassification_heterogeneity\\\"],\\n    56\\t        config[\\\"cate_heterogeneity\\\"],\\n    57\\t        config[\\\"subgroup_counts\\\"],\\n    58\\t        config[\\\"n_values\\\"],\\n    59\\t        config[\\\"expert_budgets\\\"],\\n    60\\t        config[\\\"correlation_directions\\\"],\\n    61\\t    ]\\n    62\\t    for mis_h, cate_h, k, n, budget, direction in itertools.product(*keys):\\n    63\\t        condition_id = (\\n    64\\t            f\\\"corr={direction}|mis={mis_h}|cate={cate_h}|k={k}|\\\"\\n    65\\t            f\\\"N={n}|B={budget}\\\"\\n    66\\t        )\\n    67\\t        conditions.append(\\n    68\\t            Condition(\\n    69\\t                condition_id=condition_id,\\n    70\\t                correlation_direction=direction,\\n    71\\t                misclassification_heterogeneity=mis_h,\\n    72\\t                cate_heterogeneity=cate_h,\\n    73\\t                k=int(k),\\n    74\\t                n=int(n),\\n    75\\t                expert_budget=int(budget),\\n    76\\t            )\\n    77\\t        )\\n    78\\t    return conditions\\n    79\\t\\n    80\\t\\n    81\\tdef evenly_spaced(items: list[Condition], count: int) -> list[Condition]:\\n    82\\t    if count <= 0:\\n    83\\t        return []\\n    84\\t    if count >= len(items):\\n    85\\t        return items\\n    86\\t    if count == 1:\\n    87\\t        return [items[0]]\\n    88\\t    step = (len(items) - 1) / (count - 1)\\n    89\\t    return [items[int(round(i * step))] for i in range(count)]\\n    90\\t\\n    91\\t\\n    92\\tdef balanced_limit_conditions(conditions: list[Condition], limit: int) -> list[Condition]:\\n    93\\t    positives = [c for c in conditions if c.correlation_direction == \\\"positive\\\"]\\n    94\\t    negatives = [c for c in conditions if c.correlation_direction == \\\"negative\\\"]\\n    95\\t    pos_count = (limit + 1) // 2\\n    96\\t    neg_count = limit // 2\\n    97\\t    selected = evenly_spaced(positives, pos_count) + evenly_spaced(negatives, neg_count)\\n    98\\t    return selected[:limit]\\n    99\\t\\n   100\\t\\n   101\\tdef cate_values(k: int, heterogeneity: str, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   102\\t    if heterogeneity == \\\"high\\\":\\n   103\\t        vals = torch.linspace(0.15, 1.15, k, device=device)\\n   104\\t    else:\\n   105\\t        vals = torch.linspace(0.35, 0.65, k, device=device)\\n   106\\t    return vals - vals.mean() + 0.5\\n   107\\t\\n   108\\t\\n   109\\tdef error_rates(\\n   110\\t    k: int,\\n   111\\t    mis_heterogeneity: str,\\n   112\\t    cate: torch.Tensor,\\n   113\\t    direction: str,\\n   114\\t    device: torch.device,\\n   115\\t) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   116\\t    if mis_heterogeneity == \\\"high\\\":\\n   117\\t        low, high = 0.04, 0.36\\n   118\\t    else:\\n   119\\t        low, high = 0.10, 0.18\\n   120\\t    ordered = torch.linspace(low, high, k, device=device)\\n   121\\t    order = torch.argsort(torch.abs(cate), descending=False)\\n   122\\t    rates = torch.empty(k, device=device)\\n   123\\t    if direction == \\\"positive\\\":\\n   124\\t        rates[order] = ordered\\n   125\\t    else:\\n   126\\t        rates[order] = torch.flip(ordered, dims=[0])\\n   127\\t    return rates\\n   128\\t\\n   129\\t\\n   130\\tdef transition_matrix_from_rates(rates: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   131\\t    k = int(rates.numel())\\n   132\\t    matrix = torch.zeros((k, k), device=rates.device)\\n   133\\t    off_diag = rates / max(k - 1, 1)\\n   134\\t    matrix[:] = off_diag[:, None]\\n   135\\t    diag_idx = torch.arange(k, device=rates.device)\\n   136\\t    matrix[diag_idx, diag_idx] = 1.0 - rates\\n   137\\t    return matrix\\n   138\\t\\n   139\\t\\n   140\\tdef global_transition_matrix(p_error: torch.Tensor, k: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   141\\t    rates = torch.full((k,), p_error.clamp(0.0, 0.95), device=device)\\n   142\\t    return transition_matrix_from_rates(rates)\\n   143\\t\\n   144\\t\\n   145\\tdef sample_noisy_labels(z_true: torch.Tensor, rates: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   146\\t    k = int(rates.numel())\\n   147\\t    device = z_true.device\\n   148\\t    error = torch.rand_like(z_true.float()) < rates[z_true]\\n   149\\t    shift = torch.randint(1, k, z_true.shape, device=device)\\n   150\\t    z_alt = (z_true + shift) % k\\n   151\\t    return torch.where(error, z_alt, z_true)\\n   152\\t\\n   153\\t\\n   154\\tdef observed_gate(y: torch.Tensor, treatment: torch.Tensor, labels: torch.Tensor, k: int) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   155\\t    estimates = []\\n   156\\t    for group in range(k):\\n   157\\t        mask = labels == group\\n   158\\t        treated = mask & (treatment == 1)\\n   159\\t        control = mask & (treatment == 0)\\n   160\\t        if treated.any() and control.any():\\n   161\\t            estimates.append(y[treated].mean() - y[control].mean())\\n   162\\t        else:\\n   163\\t            estimates.append(torch.tensor(float(\\\"nan\\\"), device=y.device))\\n   164\\t    out = torch.stack(estimates)\\n   165\\t    if torch.isnan(out).any():\\n   166\\t        global_ate = y[treatment == 1].mean() - y[treatment == 0].mean()\\n   167\\t        out = torch.where(torch.isnan(out), global_ate, out)\\n   168\\t    return out\\n   169\\t\\n   170\\t\\n   171\\tdef correct_gate(observed: torch.Tensor, transition: torch.Tensor, ridge: float) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   172\\t    k = transition.shape[0]\\n   173\\t    lhs = transition.T + ridge * torch.eye(k, device=transition.device)\\n   174\\t    return torch.linalg.solve(lhs, observed)\\n   175\\t\\n   176\\t\\n   177\\tdef gate_rmse(estimate: torch.Tensor, truth: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   178\\t    return torch.sqrt(torch.mean((estimate - truth) ** 2))\\n   179\\t\\n   180\\t\\n   181\\tdef bias_bound_score(rates: torch.Tensor, cate: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   182\\t    centered_rate = rates - rates.mean()\\n   183\\t    centered_cate = torch.abs(cate) - torch.abs(cate).mean()\\n   184\\t    return torch.mean(torch.abs(centered_rate * centered_cate))\\n   185\\t\\n   186\\t\\n   187\\tdef rankdata(values: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n   188\\t    order = torch.argsort(values)\\n   189\\t    ranks = torch.empty_like(values, dtype=torch.float32)\\n   190\\t    ranks[order] = torch.arange(values.numel(), device=values.device, dtype=torch.float32)\\n   191\\t    return ranks\\n   192\\t\\n   193\\t\\n   194\\tdef spearman(x: torch.Tensor, y: torch.Tensor) -> float:\\n   195\\t    if x.numel() < 2 or torch.std(x) == 0 or torch.std(y) == 0:\\n   196\\t        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n   197\\t    rx = rankdata(x.float())\\n   198\\t    ry = rankdata(y.float())\\n   199\\t    rx = rx - rx.mean()\\n   200\\t    ry = ry - ry.mean()\\n   201\\t    denom = torch.sqrt(torch.sum(rx**2) * torch.sum(ry**2))\\n   202\\t    if denom == 0:\\n   203\\t        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n   204\\t    return float(torch.sum(rx * ry) / denom)\\n   205\\t\\n   206\\t\\n   207\\tdef quantile(values: list[float], q: float) -> float:\\n   208\\t    if not values:\\n   209\\t        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n   210\\t    sorted_values = sorted(values)\\n   211\\t    idx = min(max(int(math.floor(q * (len(sorted_values) - 1))), 0), len(sorted_values) - 1)\\n   212\\t    return float(sorted_values[idx])\\n   213\\t\\n   214\\t\\n   215\\tdef bootstrap_metrics(\\n   216\\t    condition_rows: list[dict[str, Any]],\\n   217\\t    iterations: int,\\n   218\\t    seed: int,\\n   219\\t    device: torch.device,\\n   220\\t) -> dict[str, float]:\\n   221\\t    pos = [r for r in condition_rows if r[\\\"correlation_direction\\\"] == \\\"positive\\\"]\\n   222\\t    neg = [r for r in condition_rows if r[\\\"correlation_direction\\\"] == \\\"negative\\\"]\\n   223\\t    if not pos or not neg:\\n   224\\t        return {\\n   225\\t            \\\"primary_ci_lower\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n   226\\t            \\\"primary_ci_upper\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n   227\\t            \\\"spearman_ci_lower\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n   228\\t            \\\"spearman_ci_upper\\\": float(\\\"nan\\\"),\\n   229\\t        }\\n   230\\t\\n   231\\t    generator = torch.Generator(device=device)\\n   232\\t    generator.manual_seed(seed)\\n   233\\t    primary_samples: list[float] = []\\n   234\\t    spearman_samples: list[float] = []\\n   235\\t    rows = condition_rows\\n   236\\t    for _ in range(iterations):\\n   237\\t        pos_idx = torch.randint(0, len(pos), (len(pos),), generator=generator, device=device)\\n   238\\t        neg_idx = torch.randint(0, len(neg), (len(neg),), generator=generator, device=device)\\n   239\\t        row_idx = torch.randint(0, len(rows), (len(rows),), generator=generator, device=device)\\n   240\\t        pos_rate = torch.tensor(\\n   241\\t            [pos[int(i)][\\\"oracle_global_worse_rate\\\"] for i in pos_idx],\\n   242\\t            device=device,\\n   243\\t        ).mean()\\n   244\\t        neg_rate = torch.tensor(\\n   245\\t            [neg[int(i)][\\\"oracle_global_worse_rate\\\"] for i in neg_idx],\\n   246\\t            device=device,\\n   247\\t        ).mean()\\n   248\\t        primary_samples.append(float(pos_rate - neg_rate))\\n   249\\t        x = torch.tensor([rows[int(i)][\\\"bias_bound_score\\\"] for i in row_idx], device=device)\\n   250\\t        y = torch.tensor([rows[int(i)][\\\"oracle_global_worse_rate\\\"] for i in row_idx], device=device)\\n   251\\t        rho = spearman(x, y)\\n   252\\t        if not math.isnan(rho):\\n   253\\t            spearman_samples.append(rho)\\n   254\\t\\n   255\\t    return {\\n   256\\t        \\\"primary_ci_lower\\\": quantile(primary_samples, 0.025),\\n   257\\t        \\\"primary_ci_upper\\\": quantile(primary_samples, 0.975),\\n   258\\t        \\\"spearman_ci_lower\\\": quantile(spearman_samples, 0.025),\\n   259\\t        \\\"spearman_ci_upper\\\": quantile(spearman_samples, 0.975),\\n   260\\t    }\\n   261\\t\\n   262\\t\\n   263\\tdef run_condition(\\n   264\\t    condition: Condition,\\n   265\\t    replications: int,\\n   266\\t    config: dict[str, Any],\\n   267\\t    device: torch.device,\\n   268\\t    seed: int,\\n   269\\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n   270\\t    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\n   271\\t    k = condition.k\\n   272\\t    n = condition.n\\n   273\\t    budget = min(condition.expert_budget, n)\\n   274\\t    ridge = float(config[\\\"global_correction_ridge\\\"])\\n   275\\t    cate = cate_values(k, condition.cate_heterogeneity, device)\\n   276\\t    rates = device,\\n   282\\t    )\\n   283\\t    subgroup_transition = transition_matrix_from_rates(rates)\\n   284\\t    oracle_global_transition = global_transition_matrix(rates.mean(), k, device)\\n \\\"estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse\\\": [],\\n   293\\t    }\\n   294\\t\\n   295\\t    for _ in range(replications):\\n   296\\t        z_true = torch.randint(0, k, (n,), device=device)\\n   297\\t        z_noisy = sample_noisy_labels(z_true, rates)\\n   298\\t        treatment = (\\n   299\\t            torch.rand(n, device=device) < float(config[\\\"treatment_probability\\\"])\\n   300\\t        ).long()\\n   301\\t        y = (\\n   302\\t            0.2\\n   303\\t            + true_gate[z_true] * treatment.float()\\n   304\\t obs_gate\\n   308\\t        oracle_global = correct_gate(obs_gate, oracle_global_transition, ridge)\\n   309\\t\\n   310\\t        validation_idx = torch.randperm(n, device=device)[:budget]\\n   311\\t        estimated_error = (z_true[validation_idx] != z_noisy[validation_idx]).float().mean()\\n   312\\t        estimated_global_transition = global_transition_matrix(estimated_error, k, device)\\n   313\\t        estimated_global = correct_gate(obs_gate, estimated_global_transition, ridge)\\n   314\\t        stratified_subgroup = correct_gate(obs_gate, subgroup_transition, ridge)\\n matrix/provenance JSON lines for the required cells\\n     7\\t\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n     8\\t\\n     9\\tfrom __future__ import annotations\\n    10\\t\\n    11\\timport argparse\\n    12\\timport csv\\n    13\\timport json\\n    14\\tfrom dataclasses import asdict, dataclass\\n    15\\tfrom pathlib import Path\\n    16\\tfrom typing import Iterable\\n    17\\t\\n    18\\timport matplotlib\\n    19\\t\\n 26\\tCLAIM_ID = \\\"C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\\\"\\n    27\\tGRID_ID = \\\"T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID\\\"\\n    28\\tGRID_ROW = \\\"positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\\\"\\n    29\\tSCENARIO_KEY = \\\"positive_corr_high_high\\\"\\n    30\\t\\n    31\\tMETHOD_UNCORRECTED = \\\"uncorrected\\\"\\n    32\\tMETHOD_ORACLE_GLOBAL = \\\"oracle_global_confusion_correction\\\"\\n    33\\tMETHOD_ESTIMATED_GLOBAL = \\\"estimated_global_confusion_correction\\\"\\n    34\\tMETHOD_STRATIFIED = \\\"stratified_subgroup_correction\\\"\\n    35\\tMETHODS = [\\n    36\\t    METHOD_UNCORRECTED,\\n    37\\t    METHOD_ORACLE_GLOBAL,\\n    38\\t    METHOD_ESTIMATED_GLOBAL,\\n    39\\t    METHOD_STRATIFIED,\\n    40\\t]\\n    41\\t\\n    42\\t\\n    43\\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\\n    44\\tclass DGPConfig:\\n    45\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Semi-synthetic positive-correlation worst-case DGP.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    46\\t\\n    47\\t    name: str\\n    48\\t    tau_values: tuple[float, ...]\\n    49\\t    error_rates: tuple[float, ...]\\n    50\\t    group_probs: tuple[float, ...]\\n    51\\t    outcome_sd: float\\n    52\\t\\n    53\\t\\n    54\\tdef positive_corr_high_high_config() -> DGPConfig:\\n    55\\t    # Tuned to expose under/over-correction from one pooled error rate:\\n    56\\t    # error rate and CATE magnitude are positively correlated, but pooled\\n    57\\t    # correction over-corrects the high-CATE groups relative to uncorrected Z.\\n    58\\t    return DGPConfig(\\n    59\\t        name=SCENARIO_KEY,\\n    60\\t        tau_values=(0.0, 4.3, 5.4, 5.5),\\n    61\\t        error_rates=(0.02, 0.06, 0.29, 0.45),\\n    62\\t        group_probs=(0.59, 0.09, 0.14, 0.18),\\n    63\\t        outcome_sd=1.0,\\n    64\\t    )\\n    65\\t\\n    66\\t\\n    67\\tdef make_confusion_matrix(error_rates: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    68\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return C[z, g] = P(Z=z | G=g) with uniform off-diagonal errors.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    69\\t\\n    70\\t    k = len(error_rates)\\n    71\\t    if np.any(error_rates < 0) or np.any(error_rates >= (k - 1) / k):\\n    72\\t        raise ValueError(\\\"error rates must be in [0, (k - 1) / k)\\\")\\n    73\\t    confusion = np.zeros((k, k), dtype=float)\\n    74\\t    for g, error in enumerate(error_rates):\\n    75\\t        confusion[:, g] = error / (k - 1)\\n    76\\t        confusion[g, g] = 1.0 - error\\n    77\\t    return confusion\\n    78\\t\\n    79\\t\\n    80\\tdef global_confusion_from_pooled_error(\\n    81\\t    error_rates: np.ndarray, group_probs: np.ndarray\\n    82\\t) -> np.ndarray:\\n    83\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return the oracle pooled/global confusion model used for global correction.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    84\\t\\n    85\\t    pooled_error = float(np.dot(error_rates, group_probs))\\n    86\\t    k = len(error_rates)\\n    87\\t    pooled_error = float(np.clip(pooled_error, 0.0, 0.98 * (k - 1) / k))\\n    88\\t    confusion = np.full((k, k), pooled_error / (k - 1), dtype=float)\\n    89\\t    np.fill_diagonal(confusion, 1.0 - pooled_error)\\n    90\\t    return confusion\\n    91\\t\\n    92\\t\\n    93\\tdef posterior_true_given_noisy(\\n    94\\t    confusion: np.ndarray, group_probs: np.ndarray\\n    95\\t) -> np.ndarray:\\n    96\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return W[z, g] = P(G=g | Z=z).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    97\\t\\n    98\\t    noisy_probs = confusion @ group_probs\\n    99\\t    if np.any(noisy_probs <= 0):\\n   100\\t        raise ValueError(\\\"invalid noisy-label probabilities\\\")\\n   101\\t    return confusion * group_probs[None, :] / noisy_probs[:, None]\\n   102\\t\\n   103\\t\\n   104\\tdef corrected_gate(observed_gate_z: np.ndarray, posterior_w: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n   105\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Invert tau_Z = W tau_G via a stable pseudo-inverse.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n   106\\t\\n   107\\t    return np.linalg.pinv(posterior_w, rcond=1e-10) @ observed_gate_z\\n   108\\t\\n   109\\t\\n   110\\tdef simulate_dataset(config: DGPConfig, n: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:\\n   111\\t    tau = np.asarray(config.tau_values, dtype=float)\\n   112\\t    probs = np.asarray(config.group_probs, dtype=float)\\n   113\\t    errors = np.asarray(config.error_rates, dtype=float)\\n   114\\t    k = len(tau)\\n   115\\t    true_g = rng.choice(k, size=n, p=probs)\\n   116\\t    treatment = rng.binomial(1, 0.5, size=n)\\n   117\\t    baseline = 0.35 * true_g + 0.25 * np.sin(true_g)\\n   118\\t    outcome = baseline + tau[true_g] * treatment + rng.normal(0.0, config.outcome_sd, n)\\n   119\\t\\n   120\\t    confusion = make_confusion_matrix(errors)\\n   121\\t    noisy_z = np.empty(n, dtype=int)\\n   122\\t    for g in range(k):\\n   123\\t        mask = true_g == g\\n   124\\t        noisy_z[mask] = rng.choice(k, size=int(mask.sum()), p=confusion[:, g])\\n   125\\t\\n   126\\t    return {\\\"G\\\": true_g, \\\"Z\\\": noisy_z, \\\"T\\\": treatment, \\\"Y\\\": outcome}\\n   127\\t\\n   128\\t\\n   129\\tdef gate_by_label(\\n   130\\t    labels: np.ndarray, treatment: np.ndarray, outcome: np.ndarray, k: int\\n   131\\t) -> np.ndarray:\\n   132\\t    estimates = np.empty(k, dtype=float)\\n   133\\t    for label in range(k):\\n   134\\t        treated = (labels == label) & (treatment == 1)\\n   135\\t        control = (labels == label) & (treatment == 0)\\n   136\\t        if treated.sum() == 0 or control.sum() == 0:\\n   137\\t            estimates[label] = np.nan\\n   138\\t        else:\\n   139\\t            estimates[label] = outcome[treated].mean() - outcome[control].mean()\\n   140\\t    if np.any(np.isnan(estimates)):\\n   141\\t        fill = float(np.nanmean(estimates))\\n   142\\t        estimates = np.nan_to_num(estimates, nan=fill)\\n   143\\t    return estimates\\n   144\\t\\n   145\\t\\n   146\\tdef estimate_global_confusion(\\n   147\\t    true_g: np.ndarray,\\n   148\\t    noisy_z: np.ndarray,\\n   149\\t    k: int,\\n   150\\t    budget: int,\\n   151\\t    rng: np.random.Generator,\\n   152\\t    alpha: float,\\n   153\\t) -> np.ndarray:\\n   154\\t    \\\"\\\"\\\"Estimate a pooled symmetric global confusion from an expert-label subsample.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n   155\\t\\n   156\\t    sample_size = min(budget, len(true_g))\\n   157\\t    idx = rng.choice(len(true_g), size=sample_size, replace=False)\\n   158\\t    sampled_g = true_g[idx]\\n   159\\t    sampled_z = noisy_z[idx]\\n   160\\t    group_counts = np.bincount(sampled_g, minlength=k).astype(float)\\n   161\\t    mismatches = np.bincount(sampled_g[sampled_g != sampled_z], minlength=k).astype(float)\\n   162\\t    smoothed_errors = (mismatches + alpha) / (group_counts + 2.0 * alpha)\\n   163\\t    pooled_error = float(np.average(smoothed_errors, weights=group_counts + 2.0 * alpha))\\n   164\\t    pooled_error = float(np.clip(pooled_error, 0.0, 0.98 * (k - 1) / k))\\n   165\\t    confusion = np.full((k, k), pooled_error / (k - 1), dtype=float)\\n   166\\t    np.fill_diagonal(confusion, 1.0 - pooled_error)\\n   167\\t    return confusion\\n   168\\t\\n   169\\t\\n   170\\tdef metric_record(estimate: np.ndarray, truth: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, float]:\\n   171\\t    bias = estimate - truth\\n   172\\t    return {\\n   173\\t        \\\"gate_rmse\\\": float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(bias)))),\\n   174\\t        \\\"mean_subgroup_cate_bias\\\": float(np.mean(np.abs(bias))),\\n   175\\t        \\\"max_subgroup_cate_bias\\\": float(np.max(np.abs(bias))),\\n   176\\t    }\\n   177\\t\\n   178\\t\\n   179\\tdef run_replication(\\n   180\\t    config: DGPConfig,\\n   181\\t    n: int,\\n   182\\t    expert_budget: int,\\n   183\\t    replication: int,\\n   184\\t    seed: int,\\n   185\\t    alpha: float,\\n   186\\t) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], list[dict[str, object]]]:\\n   187\\t    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + replication)\\n   188\\t    tau = np.asarray(config.tau_values, dtype=float)\\n   189\\t    probs = np.asarray(config.group_probs, dtype=float)\\n   190\\t    errors = np.asarray(config.error_rates, dtype=float)\\n   191\\t    k = len(tau)\\n   192\\t    data = simulate_dataset(config, n, rng)\\n   193\\t    observed_gate_z = gate_by_label(data[\\\"Z\\\"], data[\\\"T\\\"], data[\\\"Y\\\"], k)\\n   194\\t\\n   195\\t    true_confusion = make_confusion_matrix(errors)\\n   196\\t    true_posterior = posterior_true_given_noisy(true_confusion, probs)\\n   197\\t    oracle_global_confusion = global_confusion_from_pooled_error(errors, probs)\\n   198\\t    oracle_global_posterior = posterior_true_given_noisy(oracle_global_confusion, probs)\\n   199\\t    estimated_global_confusion = estimate_global_confusion(\\n   200\\t        data[\\\"G\\\"], data[\\\"Z\\\"], k, expert_budget, rng, alpha\\n   201\\t    )\\n   202\\t    estimated_global_posterior = posterior_true_given_noisy(estimated_global_confusion, probs)\\n   203\\t\\n   204\\t    estimates = {\\n   205\\t        METHOD_UNCORRECTED: observed_gate_z,\\n   206\\t        METHOD_ORACLE_GLOBAL: corrected_gate(observed_gate_z, oracle_global_posterior),\\n   207\\t        METHOD_ESTIMATED_GLOBAL: corrected_gate(observed_gate_z, estimated_global_posterior),\\n   208\\t        METHOD_STRATIFIED: corrected_gate(observed_gate_z, true_posterior),\\n   209\\t    }\\n   210\\t\\n   211\\t    metric_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []\\n   212\\t    group_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []\\n   213\\t    for method, estimate in estimates.items():\\n   214\\t        rec = metric_record(estimate, tau)\\n   215\\t        metric_rows.append(\\n   216\\t            {\\n   217\\t                \\\"replication\\\": replication,\\n   218\\t                \\\"config\\\": config.name,\\n   219\\t                \\\"method\\\": method,\\n   220\\t                **rec,\\n   221\\t            }\\n   222\\t        )\\n   223\\t        for group, (estimate_g, truth_g) in enumerate(zip(estimate, tau, strict=True)):\\n   224\\t            group_rows.append(\\n   225\\t                {\\n   226\\t                    \\\"replication\\\": replication,\\n   227\\t                    \\\"config\\\": config.name,\\n   228\\t                    \\\"method\\\": method,\\n   229\\t                    \\\"group\\\": group,\\n   230\\t                    \\\"estimate\\\": float(estimate_g),\\n   231\\t                    \\\"true_tau\\\": float(truth_g),\\n   232\\t                    \\\"bias\\\": float(estimate_g - truth_g),\\n   233\\t                    \\\"abs_bias\\\": float(abs(estimate_g - truth_g)),\\n   234\\t                    \\\"squared_error\\\": float((estimate_g - truth_g) ** 2),\\n   235\\t                }\\n   236\\t            )\\n   237\\t    return metric_rows, group_rows\\n   238\\t\\n   239\\t\\n   240\\tdef bootstrap_ci(\\n   241\\t    values: Iterable[float], rng: np.random.Generator, n_bootstrap: int\\n   242\\t) -> tuple[float, float]:\\n   243\\t    arr = np.asarray(list(values), dtype=float)\\n   244\\t    if arr.size == 0:\\n   245\\t        return float(\\\"nan\\\"), float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n   246\\t    if arr.size == 1 or n_bootstrap <= 0:\\n   247\\t        value = float(arr.mean())\\n   248\\t        return value, value\\n   249\\t    idx = rng.integers(0, arr.size, size=(n_bootstrap, arr.size))\\n   250\\t    boot = arr[idx].mean(axis=1)\\n   251\\t    return float(np.quantile(boot, 0.025)), float(np.quantile(boot, 0.975))\\n   252\\t\\n   253\\t\\n   254\\tdef summarize(metrics_rows: list[dict[str, object]], args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, object]:\\n   255\\t    rng = np.random.default_rng(args.seed + 999_999)\\n   256\\t    method_summaries: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, float]]] = {}\\n   257\\t    rows_by_method = {\\n   258\\t        method: [row for row in metrics_rows if row[\\\"method\\\"] == method] for method in METHODS\\n   259\\t    }\\n   260\\t    for method in METHODS:\\n   261\\t        frame = rows_by_method[method]\\n   262\\t        method_summaries[method] = {}\\n   263\\t        for metric in [\\n   264\\t            \\\"gate_rmse\\\",\\n   265\\t            \\\"mean_subgroup_cate_bias\\\",\\n   266\\t            \\\"max_subgroup_cate_bias\\\",\\n   267\\t        ]:\\n   268\\t            values = np.asarray([float(row[metric]) for row in frame], dtype=float)\\n   269\\t            ci_low, ci_high = bootstrap_ci(values, rng, args.bootstrap)\\n   270\\t            method_summaries[method][metric] = {\\n   271\\t                \\\"value\\\": float(values.mean()),\\n   272\\t                \\\"ci_low\\\": ci_low,\\n   273\\t                \\\"ci_high\\\": ci_high,\\n   274\\t            }\\n   275\\t\\n   276\\t    def paired_diff(left: str, right: str, metric: str) -> dict[str, float]:\\n   277\\t        by_rep: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}\\n   278\\t        for row in metrics_rows:\\n   279\\t            rep = int(row[\\\"replication\\\"])\\n   280\\t            by_rep.setdefault(rep, {})[str(row[\\\"method\\\"])] = float(row[metric])\\n   281\\t        values = np.asarray(\\n   282\\t            [method_values[left] - method_values[right] for method_values in by_rep.values()],\\n   283\\t            dtype=float,\\n   284\\t        )\\n -0.004999995231628418，bootstrap 95% CI = [-0.08526039123535156, 0.08000001311302185]。它没有达到 positive conditions 至少高出 negative controls 20 percentage points 且 CI lower bound > 0 的 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule。\n15:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` secondary metric `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` = -0.6201611161231995，bootstrap 95% CI = [-0.6645349264144897, -0.4831351935863495]。方向与 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule 要求的 positive rank correlation 相反。\n17:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` estimated-vs-oracle gap `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse` = 0.0007658914546482265。该数值说明当前 negative result 主要不是 expert-budget finite-sample estimation noise，而是 oracle/global algebra 与 DGP/metric interaction 的问题。\n19:`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 对照：`oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse` = 0.6646851460664368，bootstrap 95% CI = [0.6574673897400952, 0.6717956725076197]；`estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse` = 0.018088719196506182，bootstrap 95% CI = [0.005477944594796951, 0.03140296703176313]。这些 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 指标解释了为什么 exp001 success 与 exp002 negative 可以同时成立：它们不是同一 algebra/weighting/aggregation problem。\n25:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` matched positive-negative pair audit：matched pairs = 96；positive-minus-negative `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = -0.004999998636776581；positive-delta pairs = 36；zero-delta pairs = 2；negative-delta pairs = 58。该证据说明有效性是局部的，而不是 H1 grid-level monotone pattern。\n27:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` condition-level mean RMSE audit：mean `oracle_global_gate_rmse > uncorrected_gate_rmse` 的 conditions = 96，mean `oracle_global_gate_rmse <= uncorrected_gate_rmse` 的 conditions = 96。worse-condition `oracle_global_worse_rate` range = [0.39499998092651367, 0.9799999594688416]；better/equal-condition `oracle_global_worse_rate` range = [0.014999999664723873, 0.4599999785423279]。\n29:局部有效 pattern 主要是 low CATE heterogeneity，而不是 positive correlation 本身：`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` low-CATE conditions 的 positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.6684、negative mean = 0.6742；high-CATE conditions 的 positive mean = 0.2157、negative mean = 0.2200。也就是说，`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 的 \"global worse\" 更受 CATE heterogeneity scale 与 sampling noise/normalization 影响，而不是受 positive-vs-negative direction 主导。\n33:- `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=6`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.8404，negative mean = 0.8554；这类条件 global correction 常变差，但 negative controls 同样或更强。\n34:- `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=6`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.8483，negative mean = 0.8612；同样是 high worse-rate but no positive-specific separation。\n35:- `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=high, k=6`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.3333，negative mean = 0.3221；这是少数 positive direction 稍高的 aggregate pattern，但幅度远低于 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule。\n36:- `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=high, k=3`：positive `oracle_global_worse_rate` mean = 0.1192，negative mean = 0.1283；在最接近 \"high/high\" intuition 的 grid cell 中，`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 反而不支持 global-worse pattern。\n38:`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` top matched positive deltas 也显示局部 pattern 不稳定：最大 positive-minus-negative pair delta = 0.09999999403953552，出现在 `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=3, n=5000, expert_budget=1000`，positive rate = 0.515、negative rate = 0.415。最大 negative delta = -0.0949999988079071，出现在 `misclassification_heterogeneity=low, cate_heterogeneity=low, k=3, n=10000, expert_budget=1000`，positive rate = 0.295、negative rate = 0.390。两者都不是 high/high mechanism 的 clean confirmation。\n42:Parameter construction differs materially. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` uses a deliberately skewed worst-case DGP: `tau_values=[0.0, 4.3, 5.4, 5.5]`、`error_rates=[0.02, 0.06, 0.29, 0.45]`、`group_probs=[0.59, 0.09, 0.14, 0.18]` from `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` uses uniform true subgroup sampling, CATE ranges centered near 0.5, misclassification ranges `low=[0.10,0.18]` and `high=[0.04,0.36]`, and `k in {3,6}` from `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`. The exp001 single point creates severe prior-weighted posterior distortion; exp002 dilutes that into many regimes where uncorrected noise can already be close to the target or global correction acts like a shrinkage transform.\n46:Subgroup weighting differs. `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` global confusion uses a group-probability-weighted pooled error, so high-effect minority groups can be over/under-corrected relative to a majority-driven pooled matrix. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` uses `z_true = torch.randint(0, k, ...)`, so subgroup priors are uniform in expectation and the global pooled error is an unweighted mean of subgroup error rates. That removes a large part of exp001's prior-weighted worst-case leverage.\n52:Oracle-vs-estimated split does not explain exp002 negative. `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` estimated-vs-oracle gap is 0.0007658914546482265, while the primary failure is -0.004999995231628418 and the rank diagnostic is -0.6201611161231995. The exp002 failure is therefore not caused by estimated confusion matrix budget noise.\n54:The stratified subgroup comparator is also a warning sign. In `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`, stratified correction has `gate_rmse=0.08234754252792217` versus uncorrected `gate_rmse=0.26032738576584474`. In `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`, aggregated stratified-minus-uncorrected mean is positive for `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=high` at 0.03885 and positive for `misclassification_heterogeneity=high, cate_heterogeneity=low` at 0.07807. Since the stratified comparator uses the true subgroup transition but the same direct `transition.T` solve, this supports the algebra-mismatch explanation.\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
